Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood(new)
Vol.2

Chapter 2492
The Controller
"That's Emperor Hanqing's face?" Hua Tianming was shocked when he saw the giant head.
The giant head was extremely fast. In the blink of an eye, it was behind Ling Shu and was about to swallow her whole.
Ling Shu's arm gently circled, and traces of brown primal fear power were released. They turned into a giant pillar and stuck in the mouth.
The head was made of mud, but its power was astonishing!
"Crack!"
Following a rumbling sound, the pillar that Ling Shu had transformed into suddenly broke, and the huge mouth trapped Ling Shu within.
"It's just a clay figurine. What's there to be afraid of?"
How could Li Luoshui allow her sister to be swallowed?
She immediately jumped up and pointed at the head with a red light in her hand.
"Whoosh!"
A crimson cone of light hit the center of the head.
"Pop!"
When the cone of light hit the head, it made a muffled sound.
Then, a thick crack appeared on the giant head. Half of the head was flipped over, and Ling Shu was able to escape.
Half of the head stopped moving, but the other half of the mouth let out a terrifying laugh.
"Hehehehe … leave the true demon body behind, and I'll let you go …"
The voice that came from the mouth was Emperor Hanqing's.
"It really is Emperor Hanqing!" Luo Zheng's eyebrows suddenly rose.
Li Luoshui raised her eyebrows, and her eyes flashed with a sharp light. She sneered: "You want to keep us here with your insignificant skills?"
"Insignificant skills … Hahahaha, then try it!" Emperor Hanqing's laughter came from the other half of the head.
"Rumble …"
The originally silent Abyss Demon Domain seemed to come alive at this moment.
Under the constant shaking, one giant head after another drilled out.
The entrance to the Abyss Demon Domain was a vast plain. Suddenly, tens of thousands of giant heads appeared on the plain.
These heads crowded together like moving mountains, flocking towards Luo Zheng and the others.
"Such power … such power …" Lingshu's jaw dropped. "The true demon body isn't the key to controlling the abyssal demonic realm. That's why Zheng 'er and Tianming couldn't control the abyssal demonic realm. Now the abyssal demonic realm is under the control of Emperor Hanqing …"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. "Emperor Hanqing shouldn't be able to control the Abyss Devil Domain, right? At that time, Han Cangyan … "
When Dongfang Chunjun used the Yin Yang Furnace, Luo Zheng did not pay attention to Han Cangyan. He thought that Han Cangyan had also been sucked into the Yin Yang Furnace and died.
Hua Tianming frowned and said, "Han Cang Yan was secretly taken into his internal world by Emperor Han Qing. Han Cang Yan is his only hope of controlling the Abyss Devil Region. Emperor Han Qing would not give up his life so easily."
"Rumble …"
In the blink of an eye, those heads rushed over like a group of running elephants.
"These mud heads can't do anything to us." Li Luoshui had no intention of retreating.
She couldn't bear to leave her sister here alone. She wanted to advance and retreat with Lingshu.
If Li Luoshui didn't leave, Luo Zheng, Hua Tianming, and Chen Huang Yijian wouldn't leave either.
"If Emperor Hanqing really controls the Abyss Devil Region, no one here is his match! I won't die. Sister, you should leave quickly! " “
Lingshu finally panicked.
But Li Luoshui was not moved. The red light in her hand kept jumping. She held a light cone in her hand, ready to hit those giant heads at any time.
Helplessly, Lingshu's eyes flashed. Not far away, Emperor Hanqing's clone held his spear horizontally. A spatial tunnel appeared and forcibly sent Li Luoshui, Luo Zheng, and the others to the teleportation formation.
But Li Luoshui had already made up her mind. She gritted her teeth and was about to charge again …
"Emperor Hanqing can sever the connection of the True Demon Body. Even if Zheng 'er and Hua Tianming work together, he won't be afraid. Sister, even if you don't think for yourself, you have to think for Zheng' er. Don't tell me you want to drag them down with you?" Lingshu said sternly to Li Luoshui.
Li Luoshui, who originally wanted to charge out, stopped abruptly …
Although she didn't know how Emperor Hanqing did it, but looking at the situation, he most likely controlled the Abyss Devil Region.
Emperor Hanqing himself hadn't arrived yet. If he could launch such a powerful attack from so far away, when he arrived, it would be even more troublesome.
"But …" Li Luoshui's face was filled with sorrow.
She really didn't want to leave her sister here to face Emperor Hanqing.
"Don't forget, I'm just a monster now. I won't die … I'll wait for you." Lingshu's expression calmed down.
She lightly tiptoed and flew back, rushing towards the giant heads. She was quickly drowned by them.
But even more heads were rushing towards the entrance.
"We should leave." Chen Huang Yijian said.
This was a helpless but correct choice. The only result of staying here was to be buried with them. After all, Lingshu could revive, but they couldn't.
Li Luoshui clenched her fists as a sense of helplessness welled up in her heart.
Thinking of her Zheng 'er, she could only make the decision to leave. She turned and stepped into a teleportation formation.
Before those giant heads rushed over, the teleportation formation flashed with spatial energy and enveloped everyone, quickly sending them into the spatial tunnel …
Just as they entered the spatial tunnel, the heads swarmed over and crushed the teleportation array to smithereens.
…
…
These few days, the Dongfang family, Mu family, Tang family, Leng family, Fang family … all of the floating islands belonging to the Wealthy Alliance were immersed in a tense and depressed atmosphere.
The reason was naturally because of the destruction of Dongfang Chunjun Huan Yu.
The guards guarding the teleportation formation were also somewhat listless …
If those rumors were true, their good days would probably come to an end.
The worst outcome could be imagined. If the Sword family and Xuanyue family jumped out, they would probably flip all of the floating islands belonging to the Wealthy Alliance into the Sea of Time.
These guards were also instructed by the elders in their family to always be on the lookout for the teleportation formation from the Abyss Demon Realm.
"Bang, bang …"
One of the Tang family's guards heard a strange sound and looked over. This strange sound came from the Abyss Demon Realm's teleportation formation. Waves of spatial fluctuations spread out from the teleportation formation.
"It's the Abyss Demon Realm. They're back! Quickly go and report! Haha! "The guard shouted loudly, which immediately attracted everyone's attention.
But the spatial fluctuations in the teleportation formation only lasted for a while before with a 'pop' sound, the entire teleportation formation collapsed!
The guards and a portion of the Tang family's disciples were also stunned.
This did not mean that someone was returning. Instead, it meant that the teleportation formation on the other side was destroyed.
"What's going on!"
"Someone destroyed the teleportation formation on the other side!"
"Doesn't that mean Lord Saint Emperor won't be able to return?"

Chapter 2493
Translator: EndlessFantasy Translation Editor: EndlessFantasy Translation
Oriental Horseshoe Crab
The destruction of the teleportation formation wasn't too serious a matter.
After all, the restriction at the entrance of the forbidden area was set up by the various major clans. After destroying it, they could rebuild the teleportation formation. However, after the teleportation formation was destroyed, there was bad news …
The Dongfang Family's teleportation formation was also destroyed.
However, when the teleportation formation was destroyed, the teleportation passage was separated from the space and cut across the sky like a blue dragon.
Within the body of this "dragon," there were a few spots of light that were moving rapidly in the spatial passage.
Because the Dongfang Family's teleportation formation was destroyed, the spatial passage deviated. Those spots of light rushed out of the spatial passage at an extremely high speed and smashed into the edge of the plaza on the Dongfang Family's floating island!
"Boom!"
More than half of the plaza collapsed, creating a huge crater.
This huge impact even caused the Dongfang Family's floating island to sink by two to three meters before slowly returning to its original position …
The Dongfang Family's guards dodged with all their might to avoid being hit.
After regaining their senses, the guards cast their gazes towards the pit.
There were four people standing in the crater …
They didn't recognize the others, except for Hua Tianming.
These years, Hua Tianming had been in and out of the forbidden area under the alias of Sky Sword, so the guards naturally recognized him.
"Sky Sword? You're back! "
"What happened in the forbidden area? How's the Saint Emperor? "
"Quick, quickly invite the clan's elders over!"
There was no need for these guards to invite them over …
This huge impact had already alerted the few people in charge of the Dongfang Family, and they were already flying over.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
These people had the cultivation of a quasi-sage, a Perfection True God, and a Saint.
This Saint was called Dongfang Horseshoe Crab. He sat on the Fusang Divine Tree all year round. He was very senior, but he rarely moved around in the world these years.
After the news of Dongfang Chunjun's death spread, the Dongfang Family immediately invited Dongfang Horseshoe Crab out of the mountain.
It was absolutely unacceptable that the number one clan didn't even have a Saint.
Dongfang Horseshoe Crab glanced at Li Luoshui and the others and asked, "Who are you? Why did you come out of the Abyss Devil Domain and step into the Dongfang Family's teleportation formation?"
Among these four people, one was a quasi-sage, two were Perfect True Gods, and one was a woman whose cultivation level could not be determined.
Although the Dongfang family had lost Dongfang Chunjun, their foundation was still there. Dongfang Huo had just come out of seclusion, so he didn't take them seriously.
Li Luoshui's expression was extremely cold.
All her thoughts were on Ling Shu …
Just as Lingshu had said, she would not die at all.
But if the Abyss Devil Domain was really under the control of Emperor Hanqing, what kind of torture would his sister suffer?
She was in a good mood after meeting Zheng 'er and obtaining a Blood Condensed Treasured Body. But now, her mood had hit rock bottom. Why would she care about this Eastern Horseshoe Crab's question?
Dongfang Liao's face darkened when he saw that these people didn't say anything. He was about to attack.
"Sky Sword! You're back? "At this moment, a middle-aged man beside Dongfang Huo said in surprise.
"Hengmin, do you know this man?" Dongfang Huo asked curiously.
The one who spoke was Dongfang Hengmin, the leader of the Dongfang Clan's Heng branch. He was Dongfang Yingqing's father and Hua Tianming's father-in-law.
Although Dongfang Hengmin was a quasi-sage, he stayed on the Fusang Divine Tree all year round and rarely came to the floating island.
This time, he was invited for the same reason as Dongfang Huo.
"Father-in-law." Hua Tianming cupped his hands towards Dongfang Hengmin.
Dongfang Hengmin was very satisfied with this son-in-law of his.
At their level of cultivation, it was difficult to find such an excellent match for their daughter.
Sky Sword's rapid rise in the past few years had earned Dongfang Chunjun's appreciation. He was vaguely on par with Dongfang Taiqing. Because of this, Dongfang Hengmin's Heng branch's status in the Fusang Divine Tree had risen a little.
Now that Sky Sword had returned safely, he rejoiced in his heart.
"Since you're back, tell me about the Wealthy Clan Alliance. My Dongfang Clan's Wu Sheng and Lin Sheng, what about them? And … "Dongfang Hengmin asked a long list of names.
Hua Tianming waited for Dongfang Hengmin to finish before calmly replying, "The Wealthy Clan Alliance has been completely destroyed. Chunjun Great Sage has also fallen. The others … other than the Sages who were sent back to the universe, everyone else is dead …"
"Hiss …" Dongfang Hengmin's expression was one of disbelief.
Everyone else's expression changed greatly as well.
Because of the collapse of Chunjun's universe, they could already imagine that the Wealthy Clan Alliance would suffer heavy losses.
But they never would've thought that the entire Wealthy Clan Alliance would be wiped out …
"Kid, speak clearly! How did Chunjun Great Sage fall? You! Why did you come back alive? "Dongfang Huo asked coldly.
Although Hua Tianming had snuck into the Dongfang Clan, he was still married to Dongfang Yingqing. Dongfang Hengmin treated this son-in-law extremely well.
Now, he was caught in a dilemma. Faced with Dongfang Huo's question, he raised his eyebrows slightly but didn't say anything.
Luo Zheng naturally felt the pressure from Hua Tianming.
He couldn't be blamed …
Hua Tianming had already done enough. In fact, he had sacrificed too much.
But this identity had to be torn apart …
Luo Zheng said loudly, "To be able to come back, you naturally relied on your strength. Chunjun Great Sage wasn't strong enough, so he naturally died. You've lived for so many years, yet you still don't understand such simple logic?"
Dongfang Huo was a Saint who had been famous for many years. How could he remain calm after being lectured by Luo Zheng, a Perfect True God? He was about to explode.
However, Dongfang Hengmin still asked the most important question, which puzzled everyone. "Sky Sword, how did Chunjun Great Sage fall?"
Hearing this question, Dongfang Huo suppressed his anger and quietly waited for Sky Sword to answer this question.
"Naturally, Brother Tianming and I worked together to kill him," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
The expressions of the guards present, the disciples of the floating island, and Dongfang Huo and the others in the sky all changed simultaneously.
Of course, they wouldn't believe Luo Zheng's words …
However, this mere Perfect True God dared to talk nonsense on the Dongfang Clan's floating island. If he wasn't an idiot, then he must have something to rely on. They also wondered what Luo Zheng's reliance was.
"Who do you think you are? How dare you …"
Dongfang Huo finally exploded. He raised his hand and grabbed toward Luo Zheng.
However, just as he was about to make a move, Li Luoshui, who had been in a bad mood, suddenly made her move. How could she tolerate this person disrespecting her son?
"Noisy!"
Li Luoshui raised her hand, and a crimson cone of light shot toward Dongfang Huo. It made a sharp whistling sound in the air.
Whoosh!
The crimson cone of light was only three inches long, but it was extremely ferocious. The power contained in this cone of light terrified this Saint.
Dongfang Huo didn't expect this group of people to make the first move. Moreover, their first move was to take his life …
At this moment, it was too late for him to dodge. He clenched his teeth and forcefully retracted his hand that was grabbing toward Luo Zheng. Then, he grabbed the cone of light with both hands!
"Crack!"
"Crack!"
"Crack …"
Dongfang Huo clawed out six or seven times in one breath.
Every claw was able to slightly obstruct the crimson cone of light ….
However, Dongfang Huo's expression became more and more unsightly. The energy in the crimson cone of light didn't weaken at all. It shot toward him like a deadly rope.
After sensing that his life was in danger, Dongfang Huo unleashed all the Dao Accumulation he had accumulated throughout his life. He formed a faint golden phantom of a large bell that enveloped him.
Dong!
The crimson cone of light smashed onto the large bell, and a melodious bell sound reverberated.
This bell sound could be heard very clearly even by the people on the other floating islands …
Clap …
However, this large phantom of the bell only blocked the cone of light for a moment before it shattered into powder.
The cone of light pierced through Dongfang Huo's chest, leaving a triangular hole in his chest …

Chapter 2494
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
Breaking the Guqin Formation
The other members of the Dongfang Family had ugly looks on their faces.
Dongfang Chunjun wasn't here, so Dongfang Huo was their backup. However, this backup couldn't even withstand a single attack from the other party …
The other secondary sage from the Dongfang Family frowned slightly as a small jade guqin appeared in his hand.
He suddenly raised the jade guqin and shouted, "Activate the formation!"
The small jade guqin suddenly trembled …
The entire floating island of the Dongfang Family was trembling in a small area.
The Dongfang Family had occupied the first floating island for so many years, and everything on the floating island was very complete.
The floating island looked the same as the other clans, but the inside of the floating island was woven with the broken branches of the Fusang Divine Tree. All the branches and the divine patterns worked together to form a huge array inside the floating island.
Someone once said that this formation would never be used because no one dared to mess around on the Dongfang Family's floating island.
However, the world was fickle. A month ago, the Dongfang Family was the pearl above everyone in the divine realm. Their position was stable, but a month later, they were in imminent danger …
"Buzz …"
As the floating island trembled, rings of light appeared everywhere on the floating island.
Almost every disciple of a wealthy family saw these rings of light. Some of the disciples further away didn't know what was going on, but they understood the meaning of these rings of light and stepped into them without hesitation.
Plop, plop …
Regardless of whether they were Paragons or ordinary True Gods, they all stepped into the rings of light and knelt on one knee. At the same time, they contributed the power of faith from their internal worlds while chanting.
"Vast Dongfang, prosper for me. Titan of Wei, control my divine sun …"
They were chanting the ancestral teachings of the Dongfang Family. Because the Dongfang Family occupied the Fusang Divine Tree, they were the first to see the sun every day in the divine realm. Since then, they had claimed that the sun belonged to their family.
The surging power of faith was quickly transmitted through the rings of light under their feet and gathered on the jade guqin in the secondary sage's hand.
At this moment, this jade guqin exploded with a terrifying aura!
This aura spread out and radiated to the floating island below …
The Han Family, the Leng Family, the Tang Family …
The people on the floating islands all raised their heads and looked up with serious expressions.
"What a powerful aura!"
"It's the Dongfang Family … What's going on? Is a powerful enemy invading? Who is so bold? "
"Heh, don't forget that Dongfang Chunjun is dead. If the Xuanyue Clan and the Sword Clan want to bully them, who in the Dongfang Clan can stop them?"
"The Dongfang family has a strong foundation …"
"Foundation? Foundation is nothing … "
Other than the members of the Wealthy Alliance, the other Wealthy Families were gloating.
That quasi-sage held the jade zither and said indifferently, "This is the first time this Pleasant Sea Smoke Array has been used since it was built … If you surrender yourselves, I can remove this array. If you resist, don't blame me for being impolite!"
Dongfang Hengmin also stood in a circle of light and contributed the power of faith from his inner world. He then said, "Tianjian, I don't know what happened in the Abyss Demonic Realm, but there are some things that can be explained clearly!"
Hua Tianming sorted out the conflict in her heart and finally decided to stand up for herself.
He cupped his hands towards Dongfang Hengmin and said, "My apologies, Father-in-law. My real name is not 'Heavenly Sword'. My name is Hua Tianming, and I come from the Great Evolution Universe."
Dongfang Hengmin was slightly taken aback by Hua Tianming's words.
In fact, Dongfang Hengmin had also suspected Hua Tianming's background.
The Dongfang Clan was very closed when it came to marriage. Most of them married their own clansmen, and they rarely married outsiders. However, Dongfang Yingqing was determined to follow Tianjian, and Dongfang Hengmin couldn't do anything about it.
Who would have thought that this brat was a member of the Dongfang Clan's mortal enemy?
"You're Hua Tianming … Then, that brat said that you and he worked together to kill our Saint Emperor. Is that true?" Dongfang Hengmin asked excitedly.
Hua Tianming pursed her lips and said seriously, "Yes."
"Shameless brat, you're full of nonsense. Since you have no regrets, I can only refine you into dust!" The quasi-sage holding the jade zither finally made his move.
The jade zither gently swayed, and the strings emitted a myriad of colors. It was like a blue mist, and it had a unique charm. However, this charm hid a powerful killing intent.
"Hehe, since you don't believe me, I can only prove it to you!" Luo Zheng stepped into the air and smiled coldly. "When I first stepped onto the floating island, I wanted to massacre your Dongfang Clan's floating island one day! However, things have changed with the passage of time, and I've already taken my revenge. I don't want to take any more lives … "
After saying this, a longsword appeared in Luo Zheng's hand.
Red lotuses containing a destructive aura spread out along the edge of the sword.
When the Dongfang Clan's members saw these red lotuses, their eyelids couldn't help but twitch …
"How can there be such a tyrannical and terrifying power!?"
"Is this brat really a Paragon True God?"
"Even a Sage would find it hard to control this power, right?"
Luo Zheng stood upright in the air, holding the red lotus longsword. He had no intention of attacking.
"Arrogant brat, die!" The quasi-sage plucked the zither strings.
The originally calm zither music suddenly became hurried, unrestrained, and filled with killing intent.
The light emitted by the zither strings transformed into multi-colored light swords that shot toward Luo Zheng!
Facing these light swords, Luo Zheng didn't dodge and allowed them to strike his body!
Dong dong …
The power of these light swords wasn't weaker than a Sage's attack.
The Dongfang Clan's formation relied on the Fusang Divine Wood to set up, and its efficiency and power were indeed astonishing.
However, they were facing Luo Zheng.
Hundreds of light swords struck Luo Zheng's body, but Luo Zheng was still unharmed.
"This …" Dongfang Hengmin and the others stared blankly at Luo Zheng in the air.
"This is a technique that can kill a Sage, but it has no effect on that brat?"
"He seems to be completely unharmed …"
"Where did this freak come from …"
The Dongfang Clan's members were all dumbfounded.
Even the quasi-sage controlling the zither was the same. However, the murderous intent on his sinister face became more and more intense, and the frequency at which he plucked the zither became faster and faster!
"I don't believe you can block all of them!" the quasi-sage roared.
However, Luo Zheng didn't even look at him. Instead, he lowered his head and said to Hua Tianming, "Brother Tianming, it's your turn …"
When the light swords struck, apart from leaving a golden halo on Luo Zheng's body, most of the energy was transferred to Hua Tianming below …
Hua Tianming nodded and also drew out Sword Tengshe.
When the first chill gathered at the tip of the sword, Hua Tianming changed the direction of Sword Tengshe and suddenly stabbed it into the ground!
Countless ice flowers circled around Hua Tianming, and these ice flowers eventually rushed toward Sword Tengshe and poured into the ground.
These ice flowers could freeze all energy. When a huge amount of ice flowers poured into the ground, the branches of the Fusang Divine Wood on the floating island instantly froze and froze …
The foundation of the Pleasant Sea Smoke Formation relied on the branches of the Fusang Divine Wood. When these branches were all frozen, the entire formation naturally broke.

Chapter 2495
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
Complicated Feelings
The halos beneath the disciples of the floating island had all disappeared.
Not only did the halos disappear, but there was also a chill coming from the ground.
Two Perfected True Gods had broken the Euphoric Sea Smoke Array just like that …
When the few secondary saints saw this scene, the astonishment in their hearts was indescribable.
Especially Dongfang Huo, who had been seriously injured by Li Luoshui.
He had only been back for a few days, but he had already encountered two such monstrous youths. There was a hint of panic and unfamiliarity in his heart. This era didn't seem to belong to these old monsters anymore.
At this moment, an emerald green figure quickly ran over from not far away.
It was Dongfang Yingqing.
She had rushed over as soon as she heard the commotion at the teleportation formation.
But halfway there, the quasi-sage activated the formation, so she could only kneel and contribute her power of faith.
After the formation disappeared, she continued to rush over.
The first person she saw was Hua Tianming.
"Tian Jian!"
Dongfang Yingqing couldn't suppress the joy in her heart.
She had been restless for days and nights, but in the end, her husband had finally returned.
"Yingqing," Hua Tianming also called out.
Dongfang Yingqing was about to rush over to Hua Tianming, but Dongfang Hengmin blocked her way. "Yingqing, do you know … who Tian Jian is?"
Dongfang Yingqing looked at her father, then at Hua Tianming. She already understood.
Hua Tianming's identity must have been exposed …
Of course, Dongfang Yingqing knew that there was something wrong with Hua Tianming's identity.
When this man had carried her and ran wildly through that abandoned city, she had already been unable to extricate herself. That was why she had taken the initiative to help him hide his identity, allowing him to successfully infiltrate the Dongfang family.
"I know," Dongfang Yingqing replied without a change in expression.
Dongfang Hengmin looked pained, and he asked again, "Then do you know that this person harmed the Dongfang family?"
Dongfang Yingqing glanced at Hua Tianming and nodded. "I, I … also know."
Actually, Dongfang Yingqing didn't know what Hua Tianming would do, but she knew that Hua Tianming hadn't spent so much effort to sneak into the Dongfang family for fun.
At this moment, Dongfang Yingqing had already made up her mind. No matter what happened, she would still stand on Hua Tianming's side.
"You …" Dongfang Hengmin's face was livid as he looked at his daughter. "Do you know that if Dongfang Chunjun really died at the hands of that brat, it would be my daughter who killed the Sacred Emperor? I … I, and the entire Hengmin family would be shamed!"
Hearing Dongfang Hengmin's words, Dongfang Yingqing's face was filled with disbelief.
No matter how powerful her husband was, it was impossible for him to kill the Saint Emperor …
She turned around and stared at Hua Tianming with a questioning look, hoping that Hua Tianming would deny this fact. However, Hua Tianming only looked at her silently and did not open his mouth to deny it.
It was true …
At this moment, Dongfang Yingqing's emotions became complicated.
In Dongfang Yingqing's heart, no matter what Hua Tianming did, she could forgive him.
But he killed the Dongfang Family's Sacred Emperor! He was the god in the hearts of the Dongfang Family's millions, an existence that could not be defied.
At this moment, Dongfang Yingqing was also stunned, and her mind went blank.
Dongfang Yingqing, who grew up in the Dongfang Family, naturally knew this …
Hua Tianming also looked at Dongfang Yingqing with a pleading gaze. Perhaps when he revealed his identity to Dongfang Chunjun, he had expected this day to come. His heart was also filled with pain.
However, at this moment, Li Luoshui sneered. "This girl is so beautiful. How could she bring shame to the Dongfang Family? I think she saved your Dongfang Family! "
Dongfang Hengmin glared at Li Luoshui angrily. "I, Dongfang Hengmin, may not be strong enough, but I won't allow you to humiliate me like this!"
Li Luoshui shook her head. "Based on the enmity between my Luo Family and your Dongfang Family, is it too much to kill Dongfang Chunjun and the entire Dongfang Family?"
Hearing this, Dongfang Hengmin, Dongfang Huo, and the others' expressions changed drastically …
Not only could she say it, but she could also do it.
"For the sake of this little girl, we don't plan to do that." Li Luoshui blinked and smiled faintly at the beautiful Dongfang Yingqing.
She knew the ins and outs of Hua Tianming infiltrating the Dongfang Family, and naturally knew that he had sacrificed too much for the Luo Family, so she stood up at this time.
"In that case, this girl will be the savior of your Dongfang Family. Am I wrong?" Li Luoshui continued.
Actually, Li Luoshui's words were completely twisted logic.
However, the so-called "logic" in the Realm of the Gods was just a foundation for strength.
When the Dongfang Family lost power, they naturally had no logic.
If they made things difficult for this little girl, they would kill their entire family. Li Luoshui was actually making a blatant threat.
Everyone in the Dongfang Family fell silent. They knew that they could not refute this twisted logic, nor did they dare to refute it.
Li Luoshui winked at Hua Tianming.
Hua Tianming walked toward Dongfang Yingqing and took her hand.
Dongfang Yingqing hesitated. She glanced at her father, then looked at Hua Tianming. She made a decision and left with Hua Tianming resolutely …
After Li Luoshui finished speaking, her gaze fell on Dongfang Huo Xie. "There's one more thing. I'm afraid I'll have to trouble you to come with me to the Fusang Divine Tree."
"What else?"
Dongfang Huo Xie replied coldly. Right now, he only wanted to send this group of malefactors away as soon as possible.
"My husband is dead and is being suppressed in the Fusang Divine Tree. Is it time to return him?" Li Luoshui said coldly.
Hearing his mother's words, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
He had been in the Realm of the Gods for a long time, but he still did not know where his father was. Logically speaking, if his father was dead, he should be reincarnating in the Dayan Universe …
Dongfang Huo Xie's eyes flashed, but he knew that he was powerless to resist this woman, so he could only nod in the end.
After healing his wounds, he stood up and led them to the other side of the plaza.
Half of the plaza where the teleportation formation was located was destroyed, but the other half of the teleportation formations were not damaged. One of the teleportation formations was the widest, and it led to the Fusang Divine Tree.
Under Dongfang Huo Xie's lead, Luo Zheng and the others stepped into the teleportation formation.
After Luo Zheng and the others left, the Dongfang Family members present sighed in relief. They had temporarily sent this group of malefactors away …
"I wonder if they'll make a move on the Fusang Divine Tree?"

Emperor Hanqing nodded repeatedly. He looked around and asked, "May I know where old Hu is? I should be able to help with the matter of removing the Primordial Chaos Seal. "
"Haha.
You seem to know everything, "Pang Miao said with a smile.
"I told you, I have all of Dongfang Chunjun's memories," Emperor Hanqing replied.
"We do need your help with the Origin Seal, but not today. Elder Hu has important matters to attend to. Return to the floating island first and receive Dongfang Chunjun's inheritance. Elder Hu will find you for the rest," Pang Miao said.
Emperor Hanqing cupped his hands at Pang Miao and the others. "You're right. I'll return to the floating island first and wait for Elder Hu's arrival."
With that, Emperor Han Qing left with Lingshu and Han Cangyan.
"Big Brother, this guy is not that reliable. He dares to test us," Pu Zhe said with some dissatisfaction.
Pang Miao was silent for a while, and then he said, "It doesn't matter if he's reliable or not. Since he came to us and wants us to acknowledge him, he's useful. Besides, the Abyss Demon Area is in his hands. For us, he's a great help."
Emperor Hanqing's arrival today meant that both sides were testing each other, but it was inevitable that they would choose the other.
"Call Elder Hu over. I have some things to discuss with him," said Pang Miao.
…
On the other side of the Jianmu.
Huyan sat cross-legged with a hook-shaped jade pendant floating in front of him.
This jade pendant was the Xuanyuan Gouyu.
Through the Xuanyuan Gouyu, the Xuanyuan Guard was able to summon each other.
Just like what Emperor Han Qing was worried about, Pang Miao and the others did not have the confidence to fight against the Nirvana Realm from the outer realm.
Over the past few days, Huyan had been using the Xuanyuan Gouyu in hopes of finding other Xuanyuan Guards.
However, the divine realm ship had been drifting in the chaos for far too long, so it was in an extremely remote location. The chances of finding the Xuanyuan Guard were too low.
Even though there was very little hope, Hu Yan would not give up. He had been using the Xuanyuan Magatama at all times in the past few days, sending his calls into the primal chaos.

In the eyes of the Xuanyuan Guard, the Saints of the alliance were their resources.
Perhaps Emperor Hanqing didn't fear them, but they didn't want to see them kill each other.
"Understood," Huyan cupped his hands in salute.
… …

They saw my ability to control the Abyss Demon Area, so they were using me. I hoped they'd help me become the strongest so I could replace Dongfang Chunjun and take control of the situation. I was also using them. "

She was thinking about how the Xuanyuan Guard would get rid of the donkey after it had outlived its usefulness. That would be the end of Emperor Hanqing, but she didn't say it out loud.

At that moment … …
Luo Zheng, Li Luoshui, and the others had already returned to the Dongfang Family's floating island.

It wouldn't be long before Luo Xiao returned with a new appearance. All they had to do was wait quietly.

The teleportation formation leading to the Takiguan Forest began to emit spatial ripples, about to swallow Li Luoshui and the others. Luo Zheng added, "Emperor Hanqing has another name. He's called Yue Yinliu."

Inside the teleportation formation, Dongfang Yingqing still didn't know what Yue Yinliu represented. She asked Hua Tianming in a soft voice. When Hua Tianming quietly explained to her, Dongfang Yingqing's face paled.

Home.

Blood Sacrifice

A month ago, the Dongfang Family was a lofty existence. Now, even a retired Saint like Dongfang Huo had to kneel before Emperor Hanqing. How humiliating was this?
As the saying goes, a man can be killed but not humiliated. The hundreds of people who stayed here had the courage to fight to the death. They were not afraid of fighting to the death, but they valued the inheritance of the Dongfang family more. They could only sacrifice themselves for the sake of the entire Dongfang family.
"Dong dong dong dong dong …"
The disciples of the Dongfang family all lowered their heads and kowtowed on the green bricks.
Cracks began to appear all over the surface of the bricks.
But Emperor Hanqing was unmoved. His expression was still cold.
Lingshu couldn't stand it any longer and said indifferently, "Since the Dongfang family has already given up the floating island, how can you forgive them for sacrificing the lives of these people?"
Emperor Hanqing glanced at Lingshu and sneered, "If those Xuan Yuan Guards kneel down for your Li Clan, will you forgive them?"
There was also a blood feud between the Li Clan and the Xuan Yuan Guard. How could they endure the pain of having their clan exterminated?
This question stumped Lingshu. She bit her lips and said, "This is different..."
"Different? Hahaha … "Emperor Hanqing laughed wildly." Do you think a floating island can be exchanged for my forgiveness? Naive! "
When the Han Family disciples heard Emperor Han Qing's words, they were stunned.
Although the Han Clan had heard rumors that Emperor Hanqing's temperament had changed greatly in the past few decades, none of them knew the truth. Even Han Tianxiao was confused.
Now that Emperor Hanqing brought up his past life, they found it strange.
"Past life, the Yue Clan? The Holy Emperor was … the reincarnation of Yue Yinliu? "
Although the Yue Clan had been banned from the floating island, there were still some things that were recorded. Han Tianxiao had read about Yue Yinliu and the Stone Forest Rat in the records.
Thinking about Emperor Hanqing's strange behavior, Han Tianxiao found it hard to believe. But he quickly understood.
Ever since Luo Zheng and the Evil Old Man released the Stone Forest Rat and awakened Emperor Hanqing's memories, Emperor Hanqing was already 'dead'. Only Yue Yinliu was alive.
Emperor Hanqing's memories were nothing more than Yue Yinliu's vassal.
The father-son relationship between him and Han Cangyan, Han Liusu and Chuyue had become insignificant. They were all just pawns for his revenge.
Even if the entire Han Clan was destroyed, he wouldn't feel too much sorrow.
Emperor Hanqing's words left Lingshu speechless …
"Dong dong dong dong dong …"
The sound of kowtowing continued.
More and more disciples of the Han Clan came through the spatial tunnel.
They came with the intention of plundering, but with Dongfang Huo and the others blocking their way, they didn't dare make a move.
But as more and more people came, their patience ran out.
Someone took the lead and started shouting...
"Old bastard, if you want to die, then die quickly. You won't know if the Dongfang Clan will be destroyed after you die!"
"Yeah, you're dead anyway …"
"......"
Why would these Han Clan disciples care about the Dongfang Clan's survival?
Emperor Hanqing had led them to the Dongfang Clan. This was a display of strength. Who told Dongfang Chunjun to die? It was time for them to be exterminated. That was what many of the Han Family disciples were thinking.
Even though some of the Han Clan disciples were moved, they couldn't stand up for the Dongfang Clan. Besides, they didn't have the right to do so.
Emperor Hanqing said to Dongfang Huo, "If you want to die, I'll grant you your wish. As for whether or not I'll spare your home? Hehe, dream on!"
Now that the Abyss Demon Realm was under his control, Emperor Hanqing felt that the entire Divine Realm was under his control. When he would settle the score with the Hibiscus Divine Tree would depend on his mood.
As soon as he said this, the strange wings on Han Cangyan's back suddenly unfurled. He flew towards the Dongfang Clan's clansmen.
The pair of black wings exuded a terrifying aura. As they swept across the bodies of the men of sacrifice of the Dongfang family, the flesh and blood of the men of sacrifice of the Dongfang family who came into contact with the pair of black wings instantly shriveled up and turned into dust.
Even Dongfang Huo and Dongfang Hengmin suffered the same fate.

Six Vein Blood Fixing Mysterious Species

Conjecture
The Blood Determination Mysterious Seed was a method to preserve the bloodline.
Every large clan in the mother world had trouble when passing down their bloodline.
As the bloodline continued to be passed down, the bloodline of the descendants would be diluted.

The appearance of the Blood Determination Mysterious Seed solved this problem.

To most people in the Mother World, such people were like legends. It was useless to ask about them.
"I'm just asking." Luo Zheng smiled faintly. "Why is the Dihong Clan so prosperous?"
"You don't even know this? The Dihong Clan joined forces with the Lie Shan Clan to defeat the Li Clan in Zhuolu. They also killed their old enemy Chi You in the Seven Flat Continents. After swallowing the Li Clan's' three continents, they naturally expanded … "Xiao Shuang said faintly.
These things had become legends that spread everywhere in the Mother World. Almost everyone knew about them.
"What, Chi You has been killed by the Dihong Clan?" Luo Zheng was stunned.
"I don't know if he's dead or not. People of that realm have too many tricks up their sleeves. However, the Li Clan has been removed from the Mother World." Xiao Shuang shrugged. "The Li Clan was once a prominent force. They had 81 Demon Gods and so many Chi You Guards. They were not to be underestimated. Even Granny often sighed at their destruction!"
Luo Zheng sighed softly in his heart.
He wanted to help his mother gather information in the Sea of True Intent. He did not expect to hear such bad news.
Although Luo Zheng had never interacted with Chi You, he was still his grandfather and his mother's father.
"You seem very unhappy." Xiao Shuang stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
The Yang Soul was a spiritual body after all. Emotions were easily released through the aura of the soul.
Hearing Xiao Shuang's words, Luo Zheng was slightly shocked.
He immediately restrained his emotions.
He did not know which force Xiao Shuang came from, whether she was a friend or a foe. Before he figured it out, it was best not to reveal his identity.
"Nothing … we've reached the first line. Let's charge over!"
A clear line appeared in the sea in front of them. After crossing this line, the color of the Sea of True Intent changed from azure to deep blue. It could be imagined that after crossing this line, the Sun Soul would have to bear even more pressure.
Xiao Shuang blinked. She was a very attentive woman and was good at grasping the emotions of others.
She had clearly felt Luo Zheng's emotional fluctuations just now, especially when she said that Chi You had been killed.
Luo Zheng did not want to find out about the Dihong Clan. He only wanted to gather information about the Li Clan.

Chapter 2526
Holding Out
Xiao Shuang was only making a small guess. She felt that Luo Zheng wasn't willing to reveal his identity, so she naturally didn't pursue the matter.
As the two of them got closer and closer to the first line, the pressure continued to increase.
Even Xiao Shuang could feel the pressure ahead.
Looking at Luo Zheng, who was still standing in front of them like an iron tower, she was slightly surprised.
No matter how shallow Luo Zheng's knowledge was, this guy was already very strong to reach this point without relying on soul pills!

Just as Luo Zheng rushed to the front alone, the people who had been wandering around also urged their True Meaning boats to come closer and follow closely behind Xiao Shuang.
With someone in front opening the way, the pressure on the people behind would lessen. These people were planning to do so.
Soon, more than ten True Meaning boats connected and formed a long dragon that rushed toward the first line.
When they were about a hundred zhang away from the first line, the north wind was surprisingly strong.
It was said that it was because of the flow of the Sea of True Meaning on both sides of the line that such a strong north wind was produced.
"Hu …"
The north wind blew against the Sun Soul like fine needles pricking Luo Zheng's body.
"Crossing the Sea of True Meaning with the Sun Soul is indeed more difficult than crossing it with the body …"
Luo Zheng clenched his teeth at this moment.
In fact, crossing the Sea of True Meaning with the body wasn't easier than crossing it with the Sun Soul. If one used the Ancient God cultivation method and cultivated to the extreme, crossing the Sea of True Meaning would be even more difficult than crossing it with the Sun Soul. In ancient times, how many people had died in the sea while trying to cross the first line? Countless people had been burned to ashes by the red sea …
However, Luo Zheng's body was far beyond common sense, so he could safely cross the first line of the Sea of True Meaning.
When they were about a hundred zhang away, the people following behind them all took out soul pills and fed them to themselves.
Their Sun Souls, which had been somewhat dispirited by the north wind, immediately regained their vitality after replenishing their soul energy.
Some people didn't have enough soul pills, so they ate three or four in a row.
There was also one person who had too few soul pills on hand. After eating one, he could only rely on his willpower to endure.
At ninety Zhang …
Luo Zheng still did not intend to swallow the Soul Elixir. Although there was a certain risk in doing so, he still wanted to see how long he could last.
At eight hundred feet …
In this long fleet, there were already people who couldn't hold on any longer.
Someone shouted, "I, I can't hold on any longer. I'll turn back now!"
After saying that, he drove his ship away from the fleet. Taking advantage of the situation, he quickly moved away from the fleet.
It wasn't the first time that most of the people were attempting to break through a segment of the line. Many of them had gone through countless attempts before they succeeded. There were even more people who had perished in the sea of True Intent during the process of breaking through a segment of the line.
Seven hundred feet …
Luo Zheng felt that his mouth was dry and his whole body was cold.
His Yang Soul Body naturally wouldn't feel thirsty. These were merely illusions brought about by the immense pressure.
Xiao Shuang did not consume any Soul Pills either. She was also holding on.
In fact, it wasn't difficult for Xiao Shuang to cross a segment of the line. With the protection of her soul magic weapon, crossing a segment of the line was as simple as playing with a toy.
However, seeing Luo Zheng grit his teeth and persevere, a thought arose in her mind. She wanted to see if her Yang Soul or Luo Zheng's was more powerful …
Therefore, Xiao Shuang did not consume any Soul Pills either. She gritted her teeth and persevered just like Luo Zheng.
Five hundred feet …
At this distance, six people could no longer persevere and had left the fleet.
The others continued to consume Soul Pills. However, the power replenished by the Soul Pills was quickly exhausted under the dual pressure of the north wind and the sea of True Intent.
"Ah ah ah …"
At this moment, a person held his head and howled in pain.
Under the immense pressure, this person went crazy. His boat of True Intent also began to crack.
"Brother Chen, quickly cast the Illusion Breaking Spell and leave here!"
"You've lost control of your heart. Even your boat of True Intent is broken!"
"Oh no, he can no longer hear our voices …"
That crazy person could no longer turn the bow of the ship back, nor could he cast the Illusion Breaking Spell to leave the sea of True Intent.
"Crack …"
The entire boat of True Intent directly cracked from the middle. His Yang Soul body directly fell into the sea of True Intent.
The moment his Yang Soul came into contact with the sea, it was like white snow meeting the sun. It melted completely in an instant and no longer existed …
"Another person died in the sea of True Intent …"
Xiao Shuang pursed her lips. Looking at this scene, her heart was also beating rapidly.
She knew that countless people had died in the sea of True Intent. However, hearing about it and seeing it with one's own eyes were two completely different things.
Luo Zheng stood at the bow of the ship. His expression did not change in the slightest.
Of course, he knew what happened later. However, he had walked through many calamities in his life. He had seen too many of such things.
Martial Dao, Divine Dao, Paramita …
With every step he took, countless living beings perished.
Those who perished in the universe were only secondary living beings. However, secondary living beings were also living beings. They had their own intelligence and souls. What was the difference between them and the people in the divine territory and the people in the mother world?
The greater the pressure he faced, the more stable Luo Zheng's heart became.
His Yang Soul was like a boulder. His feet stood on the boat of True Intent without moving. He wholeheartedly urged the boat of True Intent to move against the waves!
Three hundred feet …
The clear dividing line in the distance was already close in front of him.
"I still don't plan on swallowing a Soul Pill!"
Xiao Shuang clenched her teeth. Her stubbornness was hard to suppress. She just did not want to swallow a Soul Pill.
In the mother world, a palace maid hurriedly rushed out of the resplendent palace. Her face was filled with anxiety as she called out, "Not good, not good. Her Highness Lingshuang … Her Highness Lingshuang seems to be suffering a great calamity!"
After this palace maid called out, three streams of light immediately flew towards the palace.
"Shuang 'er has just entered the sea of True Intent. Not to mention a one-segment line, within the three-segment line, there is no one who can threaten her. What nonsense are you talking about!" After the leading golden light flashed, a burly figure berated from within.
"Old Five, let's go in and take a look first!" In another golden light, there was a tall and beautiful woman.
The remaining golden light didn't say anything and directly rushed into the palace.
The three of them looked over, and their expressions changed greatly.
In the center of the palace, a woman sat cross-legged.
This woman was elegant and refined. She naturally emitted a light aura. Her eyebrows, lips, eyes, and nose were perfect as if they were drawn by an immortal brush. Her entire person was like a jade wall, completely natural, without a trace of flaw.
Just as the palace maid said, Lingshuang's body was trembling. Her expression was abnormally ugly. Her silver teeth bit her pink lips, and dark red blood flowed out from the corner of her mouth.

Chapter 2527

Crossing a line.

With Ling Shuang's ability, even if she didn't rely on the Soul Oracle Necklace, she could rely on the Soul Elixir to cross a line.

After hearing her words, the burly man's brows furrowed. However, he still calmed down. This deduction wasn't without reason.
…

Xiao Shuang finally couldn't take it anymore.

In a short period of time after achieving the Sun Soul, she had already perfected the Sword Fate Eternal True Meaning, and then stepped into the Sea of True Meaning.
In comparison, Luo Zheng's foundation was naturally much deeper than Xiao Shuang's.
Under the dual pressure of the north wind that was getting stronger and stronger and the chill of the sea of True Meaning, Xiao Shuang felt that her solid Yang Soul was as brittle as a piece of paper. The boat of True Meaning also began to tremble continuously, on the verge of falling apart.
Xiao Shuang was unwilling to give up. She stuffed a Soul Elixir into it.
This Soul Core was also a soul body. The moment it merged with her Sun Soul, it began to release pure soul power.
This Spiritual Force gently nourished Xiao Shuang's Yang Soul, giving her a sense of solidity. Immediately, the pressure on her was reduced by more than half.

Xiao Shuang had done some research before entering the sea of True Meaning.
According to what she knew, there were very few people who could rely on their Sun Soul to break through a line. Most people would need more than one Soul Elixir!
She found it hard to imagine that someone outside of her Mother World could do it!
Within thirty meters, the pressure on Luo Zheng increased by several times.
His eyes flashed slightly. A faint golden light appeared on the surface of his soul. His gaze also became cold.
After entering the state of severing emotions, all the pressure left him.
It wasn't that the pressure had disappeared. It was just that Luo Zheng had completely blocked it. He still needed to closely observe his Sun Soul. If his Sun Soul showed signs of being destroyed, or if the boat of True Meaning couldn't hold on, he could only swallow a Soul Elixir or leave the sea of True Meaning.
Fortunately, the distance of thirty meters wasn't too far. In just a few breaths, he had already crossed a line on the boat of True Meaning.
The moment he crossed a line, it was like entering a warm room from a snowy winter. All the pressure disappeared completely.
"Phew …"
Xiao Shuang also followed closely behind. At the same time, she let out a long breath.
In addition to Xiao Shuang, there were two other people who had also crossed a line.
The moment one of them crossed a line, he was already lying on the boat of True Meaning. It was obvious that he was extremely tired. Overdrawing the power of the soul was much more tiring than overdrawing the power of the physical body. If it wasn't handled well, it would also cause damage to the Sun Soul.
Xiao Shuang stretched her Sun Soul and drove her boat of True Meaning to walk side by side with Luo Zheng. She smiled slightly and said, "You didn't even use a single Soul Elixir. It's really surprising …"
Xiao Shuang originally wanted to say that Luo Zheng must have come from some great power. However, when the words reached her mouth, she forcibly swallowed them back down.
Since Luo Zheng was secretive about his origins, she also knew to avoid this topic as much as possible.
Luo Zheng withdrew from the state of severing emotions and said indifferently, "I wonder how difficult the second line is?"
"You won't continue to attack the second line, right?" Xiao Shuang curled her lips.
"Don't tell me I still need to prepare?" Luo Zheng asked back.
Xiao Shuang nodded. "Of course! Haven't you discovered the meaning of the sea of True Meaning? "
"Meaning? I only know that the sea of True Meaning is the way to the other side. "Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders.
Xiao Shuang was a little speechless, so she said in an educating tone, "Your understanding of the sea of True Meaning is still stuck in ancient times! In fact, the sea of True Meaning is a huge place for soul cultivation. "
"A place for soul cultivation?" These words aroused Luo Zheng's curiosity.
"Yes, as long as the Sun Soul enters the sea of True Meaning, it will continue to grow. With a little growth, it can go further in this sea of True Meaning. Isn't this a process of cultivation?" Xiao Shuang said.
"That's a good point." Luo Zheng nodded. The Sun Soul in the sea of True Meaning was baptized by the north wind and the chill of the sea, and would eventually grow little by little. "But in the sea of True Meaning, only the soul can be cultivated, right? Then how do I strengthen my dantian, and how do I strengthen my physical body? "
According to what 9527 said, no one in the mother world could put their physical body into the sea of True Meaning.
In other words, after walking to the end of the Divine Path according to the "Great World Cultivation Method," the physical body couldn't be strengthened at all? Similarly, how could the inner world be strengthened?
"Have you never seen the Nirvana Realm …?" Xiao Shuang asked in a strange tone.
"I have," Luo Zheng said, "but I don't understand it."
Ming Wei, Elder Jin, Chi Yi, and the others were too strong. At that time, Luo Zheng didn't understand the source of their power.
"Hmph, wait until you reach the end of this sea one day and you will naturally understand." Xiao Shuang suddenly kept him in suspense.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and didn't ask more. If he wanted to understand the Nirvana Realm, there were many ways.

Chapter 2528
Leave.
After crossing a certain line, the color of the sea became even deeper.
In the deep blue Sea of True Meaning, sparkling stars dotted it. The soul-piercing chill was emitted by the stars at the bottom of the sea.

The two boats of True Meaning that had followed Luo Zheng across a certain line caught up.
"Friend, thank you for just now!"
A rough voice came from one of the boats of True Meaning.
When crossing a certain line, the person in the lead would bear the most pressure. The people behind would more or less benefit.
"Friend, you don't seem to have taken a Soul Pill. May I know where you are from? I am from Yuezhou …"
A male voice came from the other boat. This person's soul strength was not weak. Although he had also relied on a Soul Pill to cross a certain line, he still had the energy to observe Luo Zheng.
"There are some questions that are better left unasked. There is a rule in the Sea of True Intent. If the leader leads you all past a certain point, he or she will have to collect the Soul Dans. If you all have excess Soul Dans, shouldn't you express your gratitude?" Xiao Shuang suddenly said.
Luo Zheng did not speak. He did not know there was such a rule.
However, if there were Soul Pills to collect, he would not refuse.
Looking at this line, there would be many opportunities to use Soul Pills in the future.
He could cross a certain line without relying on Soul Pills, but that did not mean he could cross a certain line. It seemed that the Sea of True Meaning was not as simple as he had thought.
"Hehe, I did not forget this rule," the man with the rough voice said. He waved his hand lightly and three Soul Pills flew towards Luo Zheng.
The other person handed over five Soul Pills.
These two people wanted to befriend Luo Zheng. In their hearts, they had a rough understanding of Luo Zheng's background.
The first line was already so powerful. If they could befriend Luo Zheng, he would be able to help them block a lot of trouble. However, after they handed over their Soul Cores, Xiao Shuang's True Meaning boat appeared between them. "Alright, you can leave now!"
Luo Zheng didn't stop Xiao Shuang either. Although they couldn't see each other's faces in the Sea of True Intent, Luo Zheng knew too little about the Origin World. Sometimes, he might accidentally reveal some information, and it wouldn't be good if it brought trouble to the Divine Domain.
Seeing that Luo Zheng had nothing else to say, the two of them could only leave.
"You didn't answer me just now. Are you planning to go for the second-stage straight away? If you want to reach the second line, I'm willing to accompany you, "Xiao Shuang suddenly said.
After thinking for a while, Luo Zheng shook his head.
He had been in the Cultivation Pagoda for quite some time now. Although the Cultivation Pagoda had compressed time, some time had still passed in the outside world. He wondered what the situation on the floating island was like.
Now that he had entered the Sea of True Intent, he had achieved his goal of seclusion. It was necessary for him to make a trip out of seclusion.
"Crossing the Sea of True Meaning isn't something that can be done in a day. Let's stop here this time," Luo Zheng said.
"I think so, too!"
Xiao Shuang smiled gently. She couldn't wait to tell her grandmother about the Sea of True Meaning!
"Right, what is the Illusion Breaking Spell to leave the Sea of True Meaning?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked again.
Every time he left the Sea of True Meaning, he had to rely on 9527. Although 9527 was attached to his body, it couldn't trouble him every time.
"You don't even know the Illusion Breaking Spell? Then how did you leave just now!" Although Xiao Shuang knew that Luo Zheng knew little about this place, she didn't expect him to not know the most basic Illusion Breaking Spell.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. Naturally, he wouldn't tell Xiao Shuang that he was forcefully pulled out by 9527.
Before he could speak, a sharp sword suddenly appeared between Xiao Shuang's eyebrows. This sharp sword came straight at him.
It looked like some kind of soul attack, but Luo Zheng was sure that Xiao Shuang wasn't hostile, so he didn't dodge.
When the brown aura hit Luo Zheng between his eyebrows, it didn't cause any harm to him. It only left him with a complete message, and this message recorded the method to use the Illusion Breaking Spell.
Xiao Shuang's method was unique. She actually used a soul attack to transmit the message.
Then Luo Zheng began to decipher the method to use the Illusion Breaking Spell.
The principle of this Breaking Illusion Curse was not complicated. When one fell into an illusion, they could use this curse to break the illusion. However, the Sea of True Intent was not an illusion in the ordinary sense. This curse could actually break it naturally. It was indeed very unique.
After Luo Zheng finished deciphering the Illusion Breaking Spell, he found that there was a small soul imprint hidden in Xiao Shuang's soul.
Before he could ask about the origin of this imprint, Xiao Shuang said first, "Don't erase that imprint."
"Why?" Luo Zheng asked.
"The next time you enter the Sea of True Meaning, I'll know that you've come up." It was very disrespectful to plant an imprint in someone's soul, so Xiao Shuang changed to a pleading tone.
Luo Zheng frowned slightly. He was already a little unhappy.
If this imprint only recorded that he was in the Sea of True Meaning, it didn't matter. If this imprint located the divine territory, it would be troublesome.
"9527, what do you think?" At this time, Luo Zheng could only ask 9527.
"It's just an ordinary location imprint. It can't cross the Chaotic World to find you. With her ability, she can't do it," 9527 responded.
"That's good."
Only then did Luo Zheng agree not to erase Xiao Shuang's imprint.
After all, Xiao Shuang knew more about the Sea of True Meaning than he did. If they could travel together, it would save a lot of trouble.
After agreeing to leave this soul imprint, Luo Zheng began to try to cast the Illusion Breaking Spell.
This Illusion Breaking Spell was not difficult. Luo Zheng succeeded on the second try. Unlike when he was forcibly pulled out by 9527, this Illusion Breaking Spell seemed to have touched something in the Sea of True Meaning. The huge illusion suddenly collapsed in front of him. Then he disappeared from the sea with the ship.
Since Luo Zheng had left, Xiao Shuang did not stay. She also followed Luo Zheng out of the Sea of True Meaning.
"Hoo!"
In the palace, Xiao Shuang suddenly opened her eyes.
"Madam, you're finally willing to come out!" The burly man called Old Five said with joy.
Although Xiao Shuang had returned to normal after crossing a certain line, his heart was still hanging in the air. He was only relieved when he saw Xiao Shuang wake up.
"Uncle Five, is Xiao Shuang so useless in your eyes?" Xiao Shuang curled her lips.

This answer confirmed the beautiful woman's guess. Then the beautiful woman said lightly, "Actually, there's no need to force yourself. After all, there aren't many little guys in this world who didn't use a Soul Elixir to cross a line."

Chapter 2529
True Intent Remarkable Ability

No matter how strong the Sun Soul was, it would disappear completely after falling into the sea of True Intent.

What the Sun Soul feared was the chill in the sea of True Intent, as well as the north wind that blew.

As for the other form, he can enter the Sea of True Intent. It doesn't seem to be the Yang Soul, but the physical body …"
If she hadn't seen it with her own eyes, it would have been very difficult to imagine Luo Zheng's form. Xiao Shuang was only guessing based on her intuition.
After that, Xiao Shuang recounted what she had experienced.

Right at this moment, an aged voice came from above the palace. "Ling Shuang did well. Hehe. According to the rumors, during the ancient times, there wasn't any such thing as the 'Sea of True Intent'. Crossing the Sea of True Intent didn't rely on a small boat. Those who used the Ancient God cultivation method to reach the other shore didn't enter the sea with their soul, but with their physical body! Therefore, when the Ancient Gods crossed the other shore, they didn't use their Sun Soul, but their physical body! For some unknown reason, the physical body couldn't enter the Sea of True Intent, and the Ancient Gods slowly declined. Later on, there was the Great World cultivation method, which allowed them to cultivate their Sun Soul and cross the other shore … "

Then, a stooped old woman slowly walked in from the entrance of the palace.

The Grand Palace Master nodded and then said, "If Ling Shuang hadn't seen it with her own eyes, I'm afraid I wouldn't have believed that someone could cross the other shore with their physical body."
The Grand Palace Master knew a little about what had happened in the ancient times, but she didn't know the real reason why the Ancient God cultivation method couldn't cross the other shore.
"Did that person reveal his identity?" the Grand Palace Master asked Ling Shuang.
Ling Shuang shook her head and then said, "He shouldn't be from the Mother World. He doesn't even know the most basic things like the Soul Elixir and the Illusion Breaking Spell."
The Illusion Breaking Spell was mysterious, but it was the most basic soul secret technique in the Mother World. Everyone had to master it before entering the Sea of True Intent.
"Doesn't belong to the Mother World?" The Grand Palace Master's eyes narrowed slightly.
In the chaos outside the Mother World, there were some small races and forces. The scope was too broad.
"Yes, he once asked about the war between Jiuli and You Xiong, and he was very concerned about Chi You's life and death," Ling Shuang recalled.
"Concerned about Chi You's life and death? He's from Jiuli?" Old Fifth blurted out.
Aunt Ping rolled her eyes. "Just based on this, it's hard to conclude, right?"
The Grand Palace Master nodded. "Just this point, it's hard to conclude. If he could really cross the sea with his physical body, Ling Shuang would benefit greatly if she could follow him. As for what it means to cross the sea with his physical body, I still need to go to Luofu Mountain."
Ling Shuang was the favorite of their palace. Everyone hoped that she could cross the Sea of True Intent safely.
Ling Shuang tilted her head and thought for a while. Suddenly, she said, "Granny, is there a Dao of Nature's True Intent Divine Ability?"
"Why do you need a Dao of Nature's True Intent Divine Ability?" The Grand Palace Master was puzzled.
Ling Shuang cultivated the Sword Fate's Eternal True Intent, so she naturally had no use for it.
"That person cultivates the Dao of Nature's True Intent. I think he doesn't know anything, so he probably doesn't have a Dao of Nature's True Intent Divine Ability. I want to give him one," Ling Shuang said.
The Grand Palace Master nodded slightly. "Although my Palace Master cultivates the Sword Fate's Eternal True Intent Divine Ability, there are still some other Dao of Nature's True Intent Divine Ability. Let your Fifth Uncle give it to you."
…
…
Ling Shuang's guess was correct. What she casually asked was indeed what Luo Zheng lacked.
After the Li Clan was destroyed, almost no Dao of Nature's True Intent Divine Ability was left behind. Even if it was, no one could cultivate it.
This was because the prerequisite for cultivating a Dao of Nature's True Intent Divine Ability was to completely comprehend a Dao of Nature's True Intent.
After so many years, if the Xuan Yuan Guards were excluded, Yuan Chengdao's True Intent of Good and Evil in One Thought could be considered a Dao of Nature's True Intent. Dongfang Chunjun's Yin-Yang World Transformation was also considered a Dao of Nature's True Intent.
Even Dongfang Chunjun's Yin-Yang World Transformation was also something that he had painstakingly sought after in the Sea of True Intent.
After retreating from the Sea of True Intent, Luo Zheng let out a long sigh.
Although he could withstand the pressure in the Sea of True Intent, as long as he was in it, the pressure would always exist.

Chapter 2530
Changes in the Divine Realm
Inside the Immortal Mansion, Yu Shenfeng sized up Luo Zheng from head to toe. He stared at Luo Zheng as if he was looking at a stranger.

The gaze in his eyes was still sharp, but his facial features looked very natural.

In the central hall, Luo Xiao looked up at the ceiling with his hands behind his back. There was a faint trace of worry on his face.

Luo Xiao had been polishing the True Meaning of Nature during his free days.
His cultivation progress was not much slower than Luo Zheng's.
Although "Sage" was not considered a realm in the mother world, he had lived countless times longer than Luo Zheng. He had experienced more things than Luo Zheng, and his vision was broader. He had an advantage over Luo Zheng in terms of understanding the True Meaning of Nature.
Of course, even if Luo Xiao wanted to step into the Sea of True Meaning, he still needed a certain opportunity.

Luo Xiao's eyes narrowed slightly. "There's news from the floating island. The Xuanyue Clan has left the floating island."

Luo Xiao nodded.
Although there were no members of the Luo Clan in the Sea of Time, it was not difficult to plant some spies in the Sea of Time. This information was easy to exchange.
The Xuan Yue Clan was considered one of the oldest clans in the Sea of Time. They had been in the top three for countless years.
But just a few days ago, the Black Moon Clan abandoned the floating island that they had been operating for countless years. All the disciples on the floating island had left.
This matter caused quite a stir in the Sea of Time.
As a result, the first and third ranked Noble Gate Floating Islands were now empty. Everyone from the Black Moon Clan had returned to Ruo Mu.

Furthermore, Luo Xiao did not reveal the existence of the Nirvana Realm in the outer regions. The Xuanyue Clan was undoubtedly under a considerable amount of pressure by making such a choice!
However, it was not so easy for Emperor Hanqing to make a clan that had stood tall for so many years and Saints like Li Xuanyue submit to him.

After Yuan Chengdao was cursed, he turned into a skeleton and could not leave the deep well at all.
If the Divine Realm returned to the Mother World one day, perhaps there would be hope for him to see the light of day again. Unexpectedly, that day did not come. In the end, he welcomed the death of the Xuanyuan Guards.
"What about the Sword Clan now?" Luo Zheng asked again.
If the Xuanyue Clan was faced with a choice, then the Sword Clan would be no exception.
"The Sword Clan also plans to evacuate the floating island. It is estimated that soon, the Sword Clan will move the elites of the clan to Buzhou Spiritual Mountain," Luo Xiao replied.

Yu Taibai has already gone to Buzhou Spiritual Mountain. He wants to build a spatial passage. If a forbidden area is attacked by the Xuanyuan Guards, he can use the spatial passage to teleport to another forbidden area."
Like the outer wall of the Divine Realm, forbidden areas would also repel outsiders. The Xuanyuan Guards could not set foot in the forbidden area through normal means!
However, these Xuanyuan Guards could even break through the outer wall of the Divine Realm. It was not impossible for them to break through the forbidden area. Therefore, Luo Xiao had already made some preparations.
"Zheng 'er, are you confident you can cross the Sea of True Intent?" Luo Xiao asked.
Luo Xiao had never stepped into the Sea of True Intent before, but he had heard Luo Shui's general description, so he had a certain understanding.

As for Luo Zheng cultivating the Primal Chaos Secret Art and opening up an internal world, it was all an accident …

Chapter 2531
Illusion God Dao
In the backyard of Immortal Mansion, exquisite and small courtyards were arranged in a row.

Ning Yudie rested her white and delicate legs on Luo Zheng's stomach, a hint of laziness appearing on her face. Even with her cultivation and physical strength, she was still feeling a little tired after such a long period of caressing Luo Zheng's body.

Sometimes, Luo Zheng also wondered if it was too selfish to put them in Immortal Mansion for their safety.
But now, he thought this because his father had returned safely.
If it wasn't for this, if something happened to Luo Xiao and the entire Great Evolution Universe collapsed, none of the living beings in it would have survived. At the time, it was a helpless action.
Before Luo Zheng went into seclusion, Xun also mentioned that she wanted to return to the Great Evolution Universe to take a look.
In the end, she was still the queen of Yao Ye Clan and still cared about her people.
Luo Zheng also agreed to this request …
If he could successfully merge the three thousand divine daos, he would make this request to his father.
After all, the master of the Great Evolution Universe was here, so returning to the Great Evolution Universe was a very simple matter.
As Luo Zheng spoke comforting words, Ning Yudie's delicate little head shook slightly. "But this time, husband's troubles won't be so easily solved, right?"
Luo Zheng didn't reveal the situation in the Realm of the Gods to the girls.
Although they worked very hard, their talents were ultimately limited. Letting them know the troubles they were facing now would only add to their troubles.
However, Immortal Mansion had a big mouth like Yu Shenfeng. When some news reached his ears, it didn't take long for the entire Immortal Mansion to know. For this reason, Jing Wu Huan had warned Yu Shen Feng many times, but to no avail.
Before Luo Zheng could answer, Ning Yudie wrapped her arms around him. "You don't have to answer me. I don't want to know …"
…
…
The next day, Luo Zheng and Luo Xiao had already appeared in the "Fantasy Reality Domain" fifteen million kilometers away.
The last time Luo Zheng absorbed the Divine Dao, he went all the way south. This time, with Luo Xiao's help, he went all the way east.
With the power of a Saint, they arrived at the Fantasy Reality Domain after two Greater Transformations.
"Three hundred miles north of the Central Divine City of the Fantasy Reality Domain is the Dao Tablet of the Fantasy God Path," Luo Zheng said.
In the Divine Realm, there were people who had drawn up maps of the various Great Dao tablets. However, these maps were not necessarily accurate, and the Dao tablets recorded were also incomplete.
But under the guidance of the Immeasurable Ruler, Luo Zheng already had a picture of all the Dao steles in his mind, so it was naturally extremely convenient to search for them.
"Okay!"
Luo Xiao responded and stepped into the Space Passage with Luo Zheng.
When the two stepped out of the Space Passage again, they saw a huge tree floating below them.
"Eh?" Luo Zheng was slightly surprised. Logically speaking, this should not be a huge tree, but a Dao tablet.
Luo Xiao smiled faintly. "The Dao Connotation of the Fantasy God Path can create all kinds of detailed illusions. This huge tree is transformed from the Dao tablet. Zheng 'er, look carefully and you will know!"
Hearing his father's words, Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly.
Under such a careful gaze, he indeed found that there was a faint Dao Connotation flowing above this huge tree.
"Go quickly," Luo Xiao said.
Luo Zheng nodded and circled down from the sky, landing directly around this huge tree.
Around this huge tree, Luo Zheng found some bizarre things, such as a chair, a sapling, and a rock as tall as a person.
"These … are people." These people naturally did not have a deep understanding of the Fantasy God Path, so Luo Zheng could see through them at a glance.
Just as he landed, two "saplings" walked toward him. The saplings dispersed the illusions and wanted to ask about Luo Zheng's origins. According to the usual practice, Luo Zheng had to pay a certain fee if he wanted to comprehend the Fantasy God Path.
Before these two people could speak, Luo Zheng had already prepared Shenwu coins and handed them over.
This time, the number of Divine Dao coins he could accommodate was not small, and time was extremely tight. He was not willing to waste time on anyone.
He casually handed over over a hundred Shenwu coins. Logically speaking, it should be more than enough to spare.
Unexpectedly, when these two upper-level True Gods saw Luo Zheng's generosity, greed immediately arose in their hearts. They only said, "To view the Fantasy God Path's Dao tablet, you need a thousand Shenwu coins!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng frowned slightly. He did not want to waste time talking, but he had actually provoked these fellows to take a yard after taking an inch …
Before Luo Zheng could do anything, a vigorous aura came down from the sky.
When the two upper-level True Gods sensed that aura, their expressions changed drastically. They looked up and saw Luo Xiao hovering in the sky. Their faces were instantly filled with reverence.
It was not just the two upper-level True Gods here. The other people who were viewing the Fantasy God Path's Dao tablet were also enveloped by this aura. Their minds were distracted, and they could no longer maintain their illusions. They all reverted to their original appearances and looked up at Luo Xiao in the sky, trembling incessantly.
Seeing his father's actions, Luo Zheng smiled rather helplessly.
However, this method was indeed very effective. At the very least, it saved him the time to argue with these fellows.
It was difficult for this small Central Divine City to have anything to do with the wealthy families. Even if they spread the news, Luo Zheng and Luo Xiao would have been long gone. Moreover, it would be very difficult for them to know who he was.
Luo Zheng walked past these two people. At the same time, the rings of light in his eyes started to sparkle.
The Dao Essence of the Fantasy God Path was constantly being absorbed by the Immeasurable Ruler.
After a short while …
Another scale in the Immeasurable Ruler lit up, and another light appeared in the God Lattice Plate.
The Fantasy God Path had been absorbed.
Luo Zheng did not stop at all. Under the astonished gazes of the group, he flew away. Luo Xiao brought Luo Zheng with him and teleported away.
At this moment, the two upper-level True Gods in charge of collecting Shenwu Coins finally dared to move.
"Do you think that person in the sky just now is a Paragon True God?"
"Definitely! This is the aura of a Paragon True God. That youth should be his son. I think his bone age is less than a hundred years, and he has already cultivated to become an upper-level True God. I wonder which first-tier family's genius he is from … "
"But why is he viewing the Dao tablet? Could he have comprehended the Fantasy God Path in such a short time? "
"Hey, are you talking nonsense? How is that possible? Even a genius from a wealthy family can't comprehend the Fantasy God Path in such a short time … "

Chapter 2532
