Incomplete Shinto
The Dao steles in the Realm of the Gods were distributed evenly. There was one steles in almost every large region.
Occasionally, there would be two or even three dao steles in a large region.
With Luo Xiao's help, Luo Zheng often only needed the time it took to burn a stick of incense to go from one stele to another, including the time it took to absorb the Dao essence.
For two days and two nights, the father and son duo flew back and forth in the Realm of the Gods almost non-stop. In these two days and nights, they had absorbed 150 steles.
"The 151st stele …"
Luo Zheng looked at the Dao stele in front of him with a hint of surprise in his eyes.
The Dao steles in the Realm of the Gods were the result of Chiyou's painstaking efforts. They could not be damaged, or rather, they were very difficult to destroy. However, a large piece of this Dao stele was missing. Its surface was bumpy, and its lower left corner was covered with cracks.
The Dao steles of other Shinto Daos were crowded with people. Moreover, they were usually controlled by first-tier clans for profit.
However, no one seemed to care about this Dao stele. There were only a few people scattered around it.
Looking at Luo Zheng's somewhat strange expression, an old man next to him said faintly: "It's a pity that the white wall has a flaw. However, in the end, there is no perfection in all things in the world. In my opinion, the incomplete Shinto Daos are the only right Dao. Young man, can you see the meaning of this Dao stele?"
"Incomplete Shinto Daos … I wonder what kind of magical powers this kind of Shinto Daos have?"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly.
Among the 3,000 Shinto Daos, not every Shinto Daos could exert enormous power.
Some Shinto Daos were originally just a kind of comprehension of the world. However, the Dao essence of these Shinto Daos had not developed powerful magical powers.
This incomplete Shinto Daos belonged to this kind.
"Humph! Saints and Great Circle Paragons are nothing more than grasshoppers in the cauldron. Today, one grasshopper is jumping around happily, and tomorrow, another grasshopper will come out. They're all in pursuit of great divine abilities and great ambitions. But how could these people possibly appreciate the beauty of such imperfections? "The old man's face was filled with disdain." You're right, "Yang Qi said." You're right.
Hearing this answer, Luo Zheng's eyebrows suddenly raised.

We are not necessarily pursuing magical powers. they say that once you become a True God, you will have eternal life. Although we are not restricted Daos of time, we can't Of The Gods... Although we are not of the Of The Gods, can Can The Realm Can Can The God Of God will last forever?? There is no such thing as' immortal 'in this world. There is only the will that has existed since time immemorial. "The old man tried to persuade Luo Zheng again." Young man, if you can calm down and cultivate this way, it will be of great benefit to you in the future! "
Luo Zheng Luo Luo Zheng Luo? Luo Luo Luo?
In the Divine Realm, the lifespan was close to limitless. Naturally, there were many extraordinary people. Some people did not pursue great strength or supernatural powers, but had cultivated for countless years. Their knowledge and breadth of thought were not inferior to the Saints. Among them, there were some unknown people whose thoughts were even more powerful than the Saints.
It would be good if this old man could teach Luo Zheng a lesson.
Luo Zheng understood the old man's words. He bowed to the old man and said, "Thank you for your advice. It seems that each of the three thousand Shinto Daos has its own reason for existence. This incomplete Shinto Stele is also a Great Dao!"

"That's exactly what I'm thinking," Luo Zheng said.

Although this old man's cultivation isn't as good as yours, I've been immersed in this Dao for sixty Divine Eras! In the end, I still have some insights! "The old man said.
Luo Zheng nodded and walked towards the Dao of the incomplete Shinto Stele. At the same time, two golden rings of light flashed in his eyes.
The Dao of the incomplete Shinto Stele rapidly entered the Immeasurable Ruler in his mind. At the same time, one of the scales on the Immeasurable Ruler lit up in an instant.
"Weng …"
As the scales lit up, another Shinto Stele appeared in Luo Zheng's inner world!
Strands of ash-green aura were released from Luo Zheng's body. The incomplete Shinto Stele had reached perfection …
The old man standing thirty meters away from Luo Zheng was so shocked that his jaw almost dropped. He stared at Luo Zheng's back in a daze.
The other people around the Dao of the stele also looked at him in disbelief.
The reason why there were so few people cultivating the incomplete Shinto Daos was that it was extremely difficult to comprehend the Dao of the Dao. On the other hand, this Shinto Stele didn't have any powerful abilities. It was just a record of one's comprehension of the beauty of the world.
Over the years, only a few people had become middle True Divinities, and this old man was one of them.
Not only that, but because it was "incomplete", there was a rumor in the Realm of the Gods that it was impossible to reach perfection with an incomplete Shinto Stele. This was because perfection and an incomplete Shinto Stele conflicted with each other.
But on this day, a young man had reached perfection with this Shinto Stele in just a few dozen breaths.
In their eyes, Luo Zheng was even more frightening than a monster …
"This, this, this … is impossible! It's impossible to reach perfection with an incomplete Shinto Stele! Logically speaking, it's impossible! "
The old man was stunned for a long time. His eyes showed signs of collapse.
Just a moment ago, he was warning this young man about the difficulty of cultivating this Shinto Stele. Now, the young man had reached perfection and stepped into a realm that he couldn't understand.
This kind of thing really made one fall apart.
"The incomplete Shinto Stele does contain a special sense of beauty. It should be impossible to dig out a Divine Ability from it. But this Shinto Stele allows me to have a special perspective to examine this world …"
Luo Zheng was also immersed in the charm of the incomplete Shinto Stele.
When he woke up from this charm, he saw the old man's crumbling appearance behind him. Only then did he understand something.
His behavior was too shocking.
Luo Zheng didn't just walk away. With a trace of apology on his face, he walked in front of the old man and explained, "I didn't rely on my own talent to comprehend the Shinto Stele. So, it's not what you think it is … You don't have to think too much about it."
Using the Immeasurable Ruler to comprehend the Shinto Stele was no different from cheating. Causing this old man to misunderstand could be considered a sin.
However, even after Luo Zheng explained a few sentences, the old man still stood in place, motionless.
Looking at the old man's figure that was like an ice sculpture, Luo Zheng revealed a helpless expression. He still needed to absorb a lot of Shinto Stele. In the end, he couldn't waste too much time here. Moreover, he couldn't tell him the secret of the Immeasurable Ruler.

Collecting Shenwu Coins

This was something that even Dongfang Chunjun hadn't been able to do back then!
As the head of the Han Family, Hantianxiao was also the head of all the rich and powerful families in name.

These rich and powerful families had been encouraging their disciples to go to the forbidden area for so many years. On one hand, it was for their disciples to gain experience. On the other hand, it was for large amounts of Shenwu coins.
Everyone wanted to be the one to set the rules. Having a rule on the Governing Slate that was beneficial to the rich and powerful families was self-evident.
However, the amount of Shenwu coins needed to write and change the rules was terrifying. Most of the rich and powerful families couldn't save up that much money.
Even Dongfang Chunjun could only change the rules in a small area. He couldn't write a rule that was too powerful. That would cost an astronomical amount of Shenwu coins.
If all the rich and powerful families gathered their Shenwu coins, they would definitely create a tyrannical rule!
However, Hantianxiao was a little confused. Who was Emperor Hanqing and the Xuan Yuan Guards planning to use this rule against?
He'd heard that even the Sword Tribe's Nirvana Realm expert who created the Land of Non-Aggression was killed. In the entire divine realm, who could go against Han Family?
Despite his doubts, Hantianxiao had always been meticulous in completing the tasks Emperor Hanqing gave him.

Liu Linkui let out a sigh of relief and quickly left.
Most of the rich and powerful families had a lot of grudges against Han Family, but they couldn't do anything about it.

In such a vast region, the chances of bumping into the Xuanyuan Guards were minuscule.

Luo Zheng was absorbing Shinto all over the Divine Realm, but there was one person who was even busier than Luo Zheng, and that was the Evil Deities.
Currently, Luo Zheng's inner world was also a complex great world. Sects stood in great numbers, and their strengths were intricate and complex. Every once in a while, a new star would appear. After comprehending a Divine Dao, they would establish a dynasty, and even unify the entire world.
But all of them failed, and the reason was Evil Deities.
Evil Deities had become a legend in Luo Zheng's Inner World.
Although he rarely showed himself in public, the old monsters in those big sects and big forces would always advise their juniors not to provoke this person.
Over the years, Evil Deities had always been in a bad mood.

Inside this cloud, there were a few people standing on a green flying boat. This flying boat was hundreds of feet long and its surface was flickering with a layer of light green floating light. Its speed was extremely fast.
And in front of this airship, there was a huge beast running at full speed.
The figure looked like a praying mantis. It had four wings on its back and countless barbs on its abdomen. Its head was covered with thin and long tentacles. Its appearance was repulsive.
Under the cover of the Chaos Qi, its figure was always faintly discernible …
This was a Chaos Winged Beast that lived in the Chaos. This beast was extremely rare and precious in the Mother World. Especially its beast soul, it could be brought into the Nirvana Realm after being refined!
In the Mother World, anything that was related to the Nirvana Realm was precious. The Soul Elixir was one such example.
In fact, the Soul Elixir's amplification of soul power was very ordinary. There were many elixirs that were more outstanding than the Soul Elixir. However, most of the elixirs could not be brought into the Sea of True Meaning and the Nirvana Realm like the Soul Elixir. Their value was far less than the Soul Elixir. This was also the reason why the Soul Elixir could become the currency of the Nirvana Realm.
"This Chaos Winged Beast is too good at running! We have chased it for so many years. Now we have chased it to the bottom of the Chaos! I want to return to the Mother World. This godforsaken place is really not a place for people to stay! "Someone on the flying boat complained.
"So what? Once we capture this Chaos Winged Beast, our status in the Nirvana Realm will greatly increase. When we return, we will also receive great rewards. Each of you will be promoted by one star! These bitter days will be worth it in the end! "
The one who spoke was a youth in a purple robe standing at the front of the flying boat.
This youth's eyes were like torches, and his aura was extraordinary. He was clearly the leader of this group.
Just as he finished speaking, the Chaos Winged Beast in front of him suddenly let out a cry. The four giant wings on its back flashed with a green light, and a powerful and irresistible spatial force was emitted. Those four giant wings actually contained the True Meaning of Void Disillusionment!
"Damn it, it's running again. Hurry up and follow it!" Someone on the flying boat shouted.
Just as the Chaos Winged Beast entered the spatial tunnel, the flying boat followed suit. This chase between the flying boat and the Chaos Winged Beast had been going on for many years.
"Buzz …"
Just as the flying boat entered the spatial tunnel, one of the people on the flying boat suddenly revealed a strange expression. He then said, "Lord Wei Xian! My Xuanyuan Gouyu seems to be reacting again! "
Wei Xian looked at this person in surprise. Someone beside him could not help but mock, "Do you know how big the Chaos is? The Xuanyuan Gouyu's range is only so small. If you can meet the Xuanyuan Guard here, I, Cheng Jiu, will write my name backwards! "
Wei Xian nodded as well.
The Xuanyuan Gouyu could allow the Xuanyuan Guard to communicate with each other.
According to the scale of the Mother World, the distance was naturally not short. However, the Mother World was just a drop in the ocean compared to the Chaos. Could they meet the Xuanyuan Guard at the border? Even Wei Xian did not believe it.
That person saw that no one believed him, so he simply patted his waist lightly and took out the Xuanyuan Gouyu. "Then listen for yourselves!"

Chapter 2543
Communication
Wei Xian tilted his head and listened for a moment before saying, "Perhaps there are spatial tears in this cloud that are interfering with Xuanyuan Gouyu."
Xuanyuan Gouyu also relied on spatial ripples to transmit information. If there was a large spatial crack that produced violent ripples, it would indeed disturb Xuanyuan Gouyu.
"That's right. Lu Jia, you're being too paranoid!" Someone said with dissatisfaction.
Hearing everyone say that, Lu Jia felt a little resentful, but he couldn't be bothered with them.

"Sha … Sha, I am … Let's …"
Lu Jia's hand that was holding Gouyu suddenly froze on the spot.
The expressions of Wei Xian and the others changed, and their gazes instantly focused on the Xuanyuan Gouyu.

God Realm was just a conventional way of addressing people in the God Realm. This group of people naturally did not understand.
Wei Xian immediately made his decision. "Lu Jia, try to communicate with him. We'll continue pursuing that Winged Chaotic Beast. Our direction should be right, but I wonder what sort of trouble this Xuan Yuan Guard has encountered …."
They were close to the lowest level of the Chaos, the most desolate place in the entire Chaos.
He believed that no Xuan Yuan Guard would be so bored as to wander around here. They must have encountered great trouble.
If it was an ordinary matter, it would be fine. However, if the trouble they were facing was too big, they would probably have to think twice.
Lu Jia nodded and gently rubbed her finger on the surface of the Xuanyuan Gouyu. A little green light flashed above the Xuanyuan Gouyu, and then it made its own sound.

Hu-yan did not have much hope.

Anyway, it was easy to activate this thing. Doing this kind of thing was much easier than Pu Zhe looking for blood all over the Divine Realm.

Hu-yan felt that he was an idiot for repeating such words all day long.
He even missed the days when he was in Silverwing City. At the very least, he had a group of God citizens to accompany him to pass the time.
After Big Brother Pang Miao came out, he didn't have as much freedom. He didn't like to flaunt his prowess on the floating island like Pu Zhe.

Just as Hu-yan was bored to death, a rustling sound came from the Xuanyuan Magatama in his hand.
This rustling sound was very faint, and Hu Yan did not pay attention to it.
The occasional spatial ripples would also cause Xuanyuan Gouyu to act this way.

"Eh?"
Huyan looked at Xuanyuan Gouyu strangely, his ears perked up.
"Sha sha sha … I am … I …"

This was the first thought that came to Hu-yan's mind.
The Paramita Realm powerhouses outside the divine realm were like a boulder weighing down on their hearts. If they could find reinforcements, it would be their great fortune!
"Bzz …"
With a wave of his hand, Hu-yan disappeared from where he stood.

Pang Miao was completely focused on controlling the Sea Shattering Tooth to devour the blood.
This forbidden artifact had to be controlled very carefully. The slightest carelessness would cause even him, the master, to suffer a backlash.
Hu-yan's words did not reach his heart.

Hu-yan presented the Xuanyuan Gouyu.

The top three clans of the divine realm each occupied a piece of the divine tree.
The Sword clan's time in the Ruo Mu region could be traced back hundreds of divine eras.
In truth, the Sword clan was a scattered group in the beginning. Hundreds of first-rate clans banded together to form a faction.
Later on, a sage was born from this faction and completely integrated all the first-rate clans. The clan in the Ruo Mu region became a single entity and was named the Sword clan.
The Ruo Mu region was the foundation of the Sword clan, and the non-offensive areas gave them exceptional protection.
But now, they had no choice but to give up on the region.
Emperor Hanqing had expressed himself very tactfully in the Sage Hall. If they didn't join the Noble Alliance, they would have to give up the floating island.
The Sword clan had already given up the floating island, but they knew the consequences of this choice.
Leaving the floating island meant becoming enemies with the Noble Alliance.
Not long ago, the Xuanyuan Guards had killed Yuan Chengdao in front of the Sword clan's sages. The One Thought of Good and Evil had disappeared, so the non-offensive areas naturally no longer existed.
The Sword clan members living in the Ruo Mu region felt like the end of the world had arrived. Everyone was filled with depression and sorrow.
The sages, quasi sages, paragons, and countless disciples of the Sword clan had all left the Ruo Mu region and entered Buzhou Spirit Mountain. Only a few peripheral characters remained in the Ruo Mu region.
On one hand, they weren't willing to submit to Emperor Hanqing, but on the other, they chose to believe in Luo Xiao.

Chapter 2544
Discussion
If it was in the past, the Xuanyue family and the Sword family would have been enough to fight against the Dongfang family.

Yu Shen Feng floated in the air. He gently put his hands together and softly spat out a word, "Grow!"

Large trees rose from the ground.

To Yu Shenfeng, doing this was only a matter of thought.

The Mistress of the Green Demon House appeared to be a thirteen or fourteen year old girl. Her appearance was delicate and pretty, but she exuded an irresistible seductive vixen aura. Her pair of slender legs were wantonly crossed on the chair.

Other Saints might submit to the Aristocratic Alliance, but it was impossible for her to do so.

In the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, the little guy that he had high hopes for didn't disappoint him in the end. After all, he had contributed nearly a third of his energy source. Although energy source could be replenished through other means, it was still quite generous for a Saint.
After coming out of seclusion, he followed the Xuanyue Clan into the Takeshi Xuan Forest.

The three Saints from the Xuanyue Clan were puzzled by Luo Xiao's words. Li Xuanyue knew that Luo Xiao wouldn't spout nonsense, and her eyes lit up. "Why do you say that, Brother Luo Xiao?"

"Those reinforcements you mentioned are also Nirvana Realm experts?" Li Xuanyue stood up abruptly when she heard this.

They were all in the Nirvana Realm, but the difference in strength was like heaven and earth.
Ming Wei and Elder Jin had left a deep impression on Luo Xiao. They were almost undefeatable existences.

If the Xuanyuan Guards stop them outside, I'm afraid it will be very difficult," Li Xuanyue said with a frown. She really could not understand the importance of Luo Zheng entering the Saint Mountain.
Saint Jiu smiled faintly and said, "It seems that I'm afraid I'll have to make a trip to the Soul Wilderness. I'm afraid I'll have to trouble Shi Xiaoqiao with this matter. It's a pity that Gu Bei has not returned. If he did, things would be much easier …"
Just as the Saints were discussing, a strong energy fluctuation suddenly came from the Cultivation Tower.
"Bang!"
Accompanied by a thunderous sound, a large hole was smashed open in the Cultivation Tower.

Chapter 2545
The Difficulty of Fusing Daos
Fusing Divine Daos was an extremely difficult task.
Back then, Luo Zheng had spent a lot of effort to fuse 300 Divine Daos. The difficulty of fusing 3,000 Divine Daos had increased by an entire level.
Among the 3,000 Divine Daos, as long as a Divine Dao that couldn't be fused appeared, the fusion energy would go out of control.
After going out of control, the fusion energy would violently fluctuate. If it wasn't well controlled, it would explode.
Such an explosion had occurred many times in Luo Zheng's Inner World.
When Luo Zheng fused Daos, he would do it at the edge of the Inner World's Inner World.
Even so, the aftermath of the fusion energy explosion had affected the surrounding continents several times.
This time, Luo Zheng had fused nearly 2,500 Divine Daos. This was the most he had ever fused Divine Daos. However, the fusion energy started to fluctuate again.
The fusion energy was becoming more and more unstable. If this energy exploded in his Inner World, it might destroy more than half of the Inner World …
In a moment of desperation, Luo Zheng could only forcefully throw out the fusion energy.
The power of the explosion made Luo Zheng speechless.
His master had spent a lot of effort to build this Cultivation Pagoda. After all, a place that could permanently change the flow of time was very rare, even in the Divine Realm.
However, after the fusion energy exploded, it easily blew up the outer walls of the Cultivation Pagoda!
Whoosh …
Countless tiny multicolored fragments began to appear around the large hole created by the fusion energy.
These multicolored fragments were fragments of time. Because the Cultivation Pagoda's time wasn't synchronized with the outside world, after the pagoda was smashed open, the time inside and outside the pagoda started to synchronize rapidly.
"What kind of power is this? How many Divine Daos have been fused? "Li Xuanyue's face was full of surprise.
At the moment of the explosion, he clearly felt the Dao Connotation of a Divine Dao. However, he couldn't tell what Divine Dao it was.
It wasn't that Li Xuanyue had never seen multiple Divine Daos before. There were people in the Xuanyue Clan who cultivated multiple Divine Daos, and Li Xuanyue himself had also grasped several Divine Daos. However, he didn't spend time fusing these Divine Daos. On one hand, it was very troublesome to fuse them, and on the other hand, it wouldn't bring much improvement to him. It was meaningless, and he might as well focus on one Divine Dao.
Luo Xiao smiled faintly. "It's not just a few Divine Daos. Although I don't know how many Divine Daos my son has integrated, his integrated Dao Connotation should be more than a thousand."
"Over a thousand?" Li Xuanyue was taken aback. "Brother Luo Xiao, do you mean that Luo Zheng has mastered over a thousand Divine Daos? He even fused these thousands of Divine Daos? "
An ordinary True God comprehending five or six Divine Daos was already the limit. Only the elites of the rich and powerful families could possibly do so if they put in a lot of effort.
Comprehending eight or nine Divine Daos was extremely rare even in the entire Divine Realm.
Li Xuanyue's impression was that there was once a great perfection realm cultivator on the floating island who had mastered sixteen divine daos. Of course, that didn't mean that he had fused so many divine daos.
Luo Xiao casually said that Luo Zheng had mastered over a thousand Divine Daos and was even attempting to fuse them. This was extremely shocking.
"Brother Xiao, you must be joking. Hahaha, Divine Daos are complex and ever-changing. Many Divine Daos have Dao Connotations that complement and conflict with each other. Fusing two or three Divine Daos is extremely difficult, but over a thousand … This Luo Zheng is only a little over a hundred years old. Just the amount of time he has spent comprehending Divine Daos is far from enough." Zhan Wuji laughed loudly.
Luo Xiao said seriously, "I'm not joking. My son has not only mastered a thousand Divine Daos. He has already mastered all three thousand Divine Daos in the Divine Realm. It's just that he hasn't been able to fuse so many Divine Daos."
Seeing that Luo Xiao didn't seem to be joking, the Saints couldn't help but gasp.
"No wonder this brat could kill Dongfang Chunjun when he was just born," said the brothel master, who was sitting cross-legged. Her young face stared at Luo Zheng in the distance with a hint of interest.
Luo Zheng had already mastered hundreds of Divine Daos when he was on the floating island. And in the Temple of Time in the Sea of Time, after observing Chiyou's shadow, he had even comprehended the Dao Fusion Divine Ability.
Although Zhan Wuji was present at that time, he didn't understand why.
A person who had comprehended three thousand Divine Daos had never appeared in the Divine Realm. Li Xuanyue and the others even deliberately asserted that other than Luo Zheng, no one else would be able to do so.
Li Xuanyue was silent for a while before saying, "The three thousand Divine Daos are the foundation of the Divine Realm. If he can fuse all of them, what will happen?"
The natives of the Divine Realm were able to cultivate step by step to True God, Great Perfection, Quasi-Saint, and Saint because they relied on these Dao Steles. The so-called "foundation of the Divine Realm" wasn't an exaggeration.
Luo Xiao smiled faintly. "Everything will have to wait until after the fusion. No one knows now."
It wasn't that the Xuanyue clan couldn't be trusted, but there were some things that he could reveal, and some things that he wouldn't say. For example, the existence of the Immeasurable Ruler.
Moreover, Luo Xiao really didn't know what would happen after the three thousand Divine Daos were fused.
Only the second floor of the Cultivation Pagoda cracked. The third floor wasn't affected.
The opening of the Saint Mountain was getting closer and closer. Before the opening of the Saint Mountain, Luo Zheng would do his best to fuse the Divine Daos.
Luo Zheng wasn't sure if the three thousand Divine Daos could be completely fused.
However, there was one thing that Luo Zheng was sure of. The more Divine Daos were fused, the more powerful they would be …
After checking the damage, Luo Zheng didn't delay at all. He turned around and went up to the third floor of the Cultivation Pagoda.
…
…
Among the huge Chaos clouds.
Three huge Blood Runes appeared around the Chaos Winged Beast.
No matter how fast the Chaos Winged Beast sped up, the three Blood Runes also sped up.
On the flying boat behind the Chaos Winged Beast, Wei Xian was holding a big bell in his hand.
He suddenly knocked on the big bell. The three Blood Runes flew in a circle, and three blood arrows shot out at the same time!
"Puchi!"
The blood arrows looked ordinary, but their power was shockingly great. They directly penetrated the Chaos Winged Beast, leaving six small holes on its body.
"Clang!"
He knocked again, and another three blood arrows shot out!
"Yo! Wu — "
The Chaos Winged Beast screamed. Its sharp voice was transmitted in the flying boat, and the Xuanyuan Guards' faces were filled with joy.
"As expected of Lord Wei Xian!"
"After so many years of hard work, we finally subdued this beast!"
"Hahaha, this is a great contribution!"
The Xuanyuan Guards were all filled with joy.
The Chaos Winged Beast was too valuable. This was the reason why this group of Xuanyuan Guards had been searching for it for so many years.
Finally, they were about to succeed. Naturally, they were excited.

Chapter 2546
Heavensguard Pavilion
During the pursuit of the chaos winged beast, the airship received news from Hu Yan one after another.
Wei Xian now had a rough understanding of the divine realm.
Wei Xian was very sensitive to this news.
Capturing the chaos winged beast was a great achievement for the You Xiong Clan, but it was nothing in Wei Xian's eyes.
If he'd known that capturing the chaos winged beast would take so long, he wouldn't have participated in the first place.
What surprised him was that chasing the chaos winged beast all the way to the bottom of the chaos would result in such a thing. If he could bring the divine realm back to his mother world, it would be a great achievement, and he would probably receive the Dihong clan's favor!
"Clang!"
Wei Xian struck the bell in his hand again.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh!"
Three more blood arrows shot out of the blood talisman.
Even though the chaos winged beast was familiar with the blood talisman's attack, it suddenly flipped in the air to avoid the three blood arrows.
But how could the blood talisman's attack be so easy to dodge?
"Puff puff puff!"
Several more holes appeared on the chaos winged beast's body, and one of the blood arrows pierced through its neck.
It could no longer raise its head, and could only let out a low hiss. Its flying speed kept decreasing, and it was clearly at the end of its tether.
"I'll leave the rest to you." Wei Xian glanced at the chaos winged beast, then put the bell away.
At this time, Lu Jia was still communicating with the divine realm.
Wei Xian saw this and flipped his hand, and a Xuanyuan Magatama appeared in his hand.
Compared to Lu Jia's Xuanyuan Magatama, his was much more exquisite.
As the Xuanyuan Magatama was activated, Wei Xian said, "I am Wei Xian, may I ask who your leader is?"
In the divine realm …
The sound that was originally intermittent suddenly became clear.
Wei Xian's words were transmitted to Pang Miao and Hu Yan's ears.
"Master Wei Xian! It's Master Wei Xian! "
Pang Miao's face lit up with joy, while Hu Yan looked confused, because he'd never heard of this person.
However, seeing how agitated Pang Miao was, this Wei Xian was definitely not an ordinary person. Huyan naturally did not dare to ask.
"I'm Pang Miao, a member of the Three-Star Xuanyuan Guard. I belong to the Gold-Destroying City …" Pang Miao immediately revealed his background.
Hearing Pang Miao's self-introduction, Fu Xian's voice continued to sound out. "If there's no Nirvana Realm powerhouse in the God Territory, then why do you still need to seek help?"
From Wei Xian's point of view, if Pang Miao and the other Xuan Yuan Guards could deal with the Divine Realm, they would definitely bring the Divine Realm back to their homeworld without a word. No one was an idiot. There was also competition between the Xuan Yuan Guards, and the competition was extremely intense.
Since they chose to ask for help, they must have encountered some trouble.
Wei Xian was a very cautious person. Although he was confident in his strength, anything could happen in this vast chaos, especially in the God Territory, which was a world created by Chi You. With Chi You's ability, even if he was nailed to death, killing a few Nirvana Realm powerhouses in the God Territory was as easy as swatting flies. Wei Xian would not put himself in danger so easily.
Pang Miao immediately understood what Wei Xian meant, and immediately said, "Originally, there was no need to ask for help, but a group of Nirvana Realm powerhouses suddenly came from outside the God Territory. There were even Five-Star Chi You Guards among them!"
"Five-Star Chi You Guards …" Wei Xian raised his eyebrows slightly.
Stars were just a title. No one knew when it had become popular. The Youxiong Clan had the Xuan Yuan Guards, the Jiuli Clan had the Chi You Guards, but the stars only reflected the person's contributions to the clan.
However, a Chi You Guard that could reach Five-Star would definitely be powerful.
"How many are there?" Wei Xian asked again.
"About ten. It's said that some of them belong to the Nuwa Clan," Pang Miao continued.
Wei Xian nodded. It didn't matter which faction they belonged to. Once Wei Xian made up his mind, everyone would be killed by him.
Pang Miao was probably worried that Wei Xian wouldn't make a move, so he quickly said, "Lord Wei Xian, these Nirvana Realm powerhouses are just a mob in front of you. If you can come personally, you can easily wipe them out …"
"I understand. I'll track your location." After Wei Xian finished speaking, the light on the upper layer of the Xuanyuan Magatama disappeared.
Lu Jia asked, "Are we going to the God Territory?"
Wei Xian put his hands behind his back and pondered for a while. "Five-Star Chi You Guards. If we encounter those extremely powerful ones, it'll be a little troublesome. However, the elites of the Jiuli Clan were all killed in Zhuolu. Those who wandered outside the mother world shouldn't be those core figures … Let's go!"
As he spoke, the Chaos Winged Beast in front of the flying ship suddenly exploded with a shocking aura before it died.
The Xuan Yuan Guards had originally received Wei Xian's order to kill the dying Chaos Winged Beast. They didn't expect the Chaos Winged Beast's counterattack to be so terrifying before it died.
The originally shriveled and flat pair of wings suddenly swelled. The Chaos Winged Beast had already gained intelligence. Knowing that it couldn't escape, it chose to die together with them.
The Xuan Yuan Guards also showed panic on their faces. All of them wanted to retreat at the last minute.
Seeing this scene, Wei Xian leaped up with an indifferent expression. A golden dot of light appeared in his hand.
"After chasing you for so long, I'll have to end your life with my own hands …"
This golden light transformed into a golden sword and slashed down at the Chaos Winged Beast's head. The Chaos Winged Beast was already at the end of its rope. How could it block Wei Xian's attack?
Its body that had just swelled up was instantly pierced by the golden sword. A huge wound spread from its back to its head. Only then did the Chaos Winged Beast die.

However, there were so many variables in the divine realm. There were also some things he didn't know. The result of such a divination was often inaccurate.

"I told you, they'll definitely escape if they have strong reinforcements," Huyan said flatly.

Universe after universe stood in the sky above the divine realm, but no one was allowed to attack directly. Otherwise, they'd be severely punished by the Governing Slate.
There were strong and weak rules on the Governing Slate, and some were added by later generations.
However, the rules of the universe were very specific. They'd existed since the Governing Slate's creation, and could be said to have been personally created by Chiyou.
These rules were very difficult to erase. Even when Dongfang Chunjun changed a word in the Universal War, he'd spent quite a bit of shenwu coins.
If Emperor Hanqing wanted to erase the rules of the universe, he'd have to spend a shocking amount of shenwu coins.
After Dongfang Chunjun's fall, Han Jia forced all the noble houses to pay shenwu coins. The noble houses were pressured into collecting shenwu coins from the first- and second-tier houses within their borders. It could be said that the majority of shenwu coins in the divine realm had been collected by Han Jia.
Emperor Hanqing could indeed remove this restriction.
When the other Sages heard Emperor Hanqing's words, their expressions changed.
The reason why Saints rarely fought to the death was because of the rules on the Governing Slate. If they erased the rule that forbade them from attacking the universe, then Saints could wantonly destroy the universe of their opponent, easily taking their opponent's life. If that happened, then the entire Divine Realm would probably be thrown into chaos.
"Don't even think about it! The shenwu coins your Han Jia plundered don't belong to you. They belong to Big Brother Pang Miao, "said Huyan." They're just some Saints. If they escape, then so be it. Don't waste these shenwu coins on them. Hehe, they don't have the right! "
Although Huyan had found strong reinforcements through Xuanyuan Gouyu, they were still afraid of accidents. For example, the Chiyou Wei had broken through the spatial barrier in advance.
Pang Miao's forbidden weapon, the Sea Splitting Tooth, was already prepared. However, with the protection of the Governing Slate's rules, he would have a higher chance of winning. How could he allow himself to waste Shenwu Coins on a Saint?
Seeing Emperor Hanqing's unwilling expression, Huyan patted him on the shoulder. "Alright, Big Brother has already arranged everything. These Saints, including the Paramita realm powerhouses from the outer realms, can forget about leaving alive!"
Emperor Hanqing's eyebrows twitched at this.
He knew very well that the Xuanyuan Wei was extremely afraid of the outer realms. That was why they were doing everything they could to set up a trap. For example, letting the Sea Splitting Tooth devour blood, forming an alliance of powerful families, writing new rules for the Governing Slate, and so on.
Now that Huyan had suddenly become so confident, he naturally found it strange. "Big Brother Pang Miao has already thought of a way to deal with them?"
Huyan smiled and nodded. "Don't ask what you shouldn't ask. It won't be long before you find out!"
This reply calmed Emperor Hanqing down.
He originally wanted to attack the Soul Wilderness while he was at it, but in the end, he gave up on the idea.
… …
… …
Two months passed quickly.
During this time, more and more quasi saints and perfect true gods rushed to Saint Mountain.
"Saint Mountain should open in three days!"
"There really isn't any movement from the powerful families this time. Looks like the rumors are true!"
"This way, our chances have indeed increased greatly!"
The perfect true gods and quasi saints stared at Saint Mountain as they discussed.
Even though they could get along well with each other now, one more person meant one more competition.
For the sake of a Saint's position, two brothers had killed each other before.
At this moment, some black dots suddenly flew over from not far away.
"That's the perfect true gods of the Bai family … Damn it, they still came!"
"Who said that the powerful families won't fight for the Saint's position?"
"But this time, there seems to be much fewer people than in previous years."
The black dots rapidly approached and landed on the other side of the empty space.
Dongfang Chunjun's perfect true gods and quasi saints in the alliance of powerful families had almost all died in the Abyss Devil Zone. Only those who stayed behind to guard the floating island had luckily escaped, so the number of people sent by the powerful families was naturally much lower.
In the past, the quasi saints and perfect true gods of the powerful families were usually overbearing, but this time, everyone had a gloomy face and did not say a word.
These people of the powerful families had all received the same order. Aside from fighting for the Saint's position, their main goal was to kill Hua Tianming and Luo Zheng.
Some news of Hua Tianming and Luo Zheng from the battle in the Abyss Devil Zone had already been spread by the resurrected Saints. These two people were far more powerful than Saints. Even Saints would find it difficult to fight against them. Wasn't it courting death for them to fight against these two people?
However, even the Saints of their families could not disobey Emperor Han Qing's order. How could these quasi saints and perfect true gods refuse?
So their gloomy mood was overflowing …
However, they also heard from their Saints that the alliance of powerful families would do their best to stop these two people from entering Saint Mountain. If they could successfully stop them, they would naturally have nothing to do.
In these three days, everything seemed calm, but various forces were secretly surging.
In the Sea of Time, Yu Xiu had already awakened Di Hen …
In the mountains thousands of miles north of the Sea of Time, Shi Xiaoqiao and Jiu Sheng were hiding.
In the outer region of the Divine Zone, Ming Wei personally brought Luo Zheng and Hua Tianming, as well as Chen Huang Yijian to the center of the Divine Zone.
Of course, Luo Xiao and the other Saints also went together.
All the positions had already been determined. When Saint Mountain opened, Jiu Sheng would immediately observe it and inform Luo Xiao.
Ming Wei stretched out her hand and lightly pointed. The True Meaning of Void Disillusionment bloomed at her fingertips, and a circle of spatial fluctuations gently enveloped the surface of the Divine Zone.

After about the time it takes to burn a stick of incense, he felt the silver light gradually dissipate. Only then did he vaguely see the road ahead. It was an open silver valley.

"We're finally in," Hua Tianming said.
"It's really scary but not dangerous," Chen Huang Yijian said with lingering fear.

If it were any other Nirvana Realm expert, their physical body would probably be crushed into a pool of blood by Ming Wei's finger.
However, in the face of danger, Wei Xian had actually abandoned his own life force and turned himself into a bloody corpse. Strictly speaking, he was now a dead man. In the state of a bloody corpse, his physical body would not be damaged at all, except for a few methods!

The End
Luo Zheng didn't know how deep the tunnel was. Perhaps he had already reached the core area of the maze.

Or it could be said that Luo Zheng would have already died in the time that the Time Imperial Jade Seal erased.
He could only use the ability of the Time Imperial Jade Seal once in a short period of time. He couldn't waste it casually …
Just as the spiral in front of him rushed toward Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng turned his shoulder and drilled into the continuous space-time on the wall of the tunnel.
This time was different from the time he entered the Time Imperial Jade Seal. The moment he drilled into it, he seemed to have arrived in another world. A strong wind blew on his face, and fine sand also rushed toward him!
These sand grains were actually colorful particles. Each grain of sand contained an astonishing cutting power. When they hit Luo Zheng's body, he immediately felt a sharp pain on his face!
Luo Zheng covered his face. After careful observation, he discovered that this was a desert that wasn't very wide. The entire desert was made up of colorful sand.
"This sand …"
He slowly squatted down and stretched out his hand to touch it. Immediately, he felt a sharp pain on his fingertips.
If it weren't for his sturdy body, he would have been riddled with holes by these sand grains.
"What the hell is this strange place …"
Just as Luo Zheng was puzzled, a loud cry suddenly came from the center of the desert. That cry was like the cry of a baby.
From the moment he heard the first cry, Luo Zheng associated it with the breathing and laughter coming from the tunnel. If there really was a baby, then the baby in the desert was probably the same as the baby in the maze.
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng jumped up against the wind and sand. Then, he saw an astonishing scene.
In the center of the desert was a low basin formed by circular sand dunes. A golden cross-shaped pattern floated in the middle of the basin. Below the cross-shaped pattern, there was a faint shadow of a baby.
The baby's shadow exuded a powerful aura, as if it wanted to completely condense into a shape.
However, the sand formed by time formed huge five-colored tornadoes that kept sweeping over the baby's body.
Every time the baby tried to condense into a shape, the five-colored tornadoes would roll towards the baby and tear the baby's shadow to pieces, preventing it from condensing into a shape.
"Wah, wah …"
The baby seemed to be very stubborn. It cried as it reformed.
The speed of his condensation became faster and faster, while the tearing speed of the five-colored tornadoes became slower and slower.
"If this continues, the baby will take shape sooner or later," Luo Zheng said in the air.
Just as Luo Zheng had predicted, the baby's shadow became more and more solid. Luo Zheng could almost see the baby's smooth skin. The cross-shaped pattern floating above the baby's head slowly descended and was about to stick to the baby's forehead.
But at this moment, a huge mouth suddenly opened in the dark sky, and a vast amount of Time Seawater poured down from the mouth.
All the Time Seawater poured down on the baby's body. The Time Seawater destroyed the baby's condensed body without any suspense, and the excess Time Seawater seeped into the desert.
Although the baby's shadow was destroyed, the cross-shaped pattern on its head did not disappear. It was still floating quietly in the center of the basin.
Luo Zheng originally wanted to observe for a while, but the five-colored tornadoes that were raging in the basin seemed to have discovered Luo Zheng and flew towards him at an incredible speed.
Although Luo Zheng was not afraid of the sand formed by time, the situation would be different if he was swept into the tornadoes.
Luo Zheng jumped in fright, and without any hesitation, he shot down.
He could still see the complete space-time wall. The moment he drilled through the wall, he returned to the corridor.
Only then did Luo Zheng let out a deep breath.
The scene that happened in the desert should have happened in the past. What did the baby's shadow represent? Why would the Time Seawater pour down on its body?
Just as Luo Zheng returned to the corridor, the giggling sound was heard again.
"Rumble …"
In the depths of the corridor, there was another spiral rushing over.
Since the space-time walls on both sides of the corridor could be entered and exited at will, Luo Zheng did not panic at all.
This time, he deliberately chose the wall on the left, wanting to see what was in the space-time on the left.
Unexpectedly, after he drilled into the space-time, he was greeted by the same five-colored sandstorm. The scene inside was exactly the same as the one on the right. It was a small desert with a ring-shaped hill. In the basin below the hill, there was a cross-shaped pattern and a baby's shadow.
The scene that Luo Zheng saw before reappeared in front of Luo Zheng's eyes. It was another baby struggling and crying under several colorful tornadoes.
"Is it the same space-time?"
Luo Zheng was suspicious, but when he saw the baby's shadow struggling, Luo Zheng immediately rejected this idea.
Although it was the same baby's shadow, the way it struggled was completely different from before. This showed that this kind of thing had happened more than once in the desert!
After the baby's shadow struggled for a long time, just as it was about to take shape, Luo Zheng subconsciously looked up at the pitch-black sky above.
Just as Luo Zheng had guessed, another big mouth opened in the sky. The Time Seawater poured down from the sky and poured down on the baby's body.
Luo Zheng just shook his head and returned to the corridor.
At this stage, he did not know what this meant. Fortunately, he could use the space-time walls on both sides of the corridor to avoid the spiral. This way, he could still move forward according to the Immeasurable Ruler's instructions.
"Giggle …"
As Luo Zheng moved forward in the corridor, the baby's laughter moved forward in his ears.
But now, he had calmly drilled into the space-time wall. After the spiral left, Luo Zheng returned to the corridor again.
Shuttling back and forth like this, Luo Zheng became more and more proficient.
Although the baby's strange laughter became more and more frequent, and the spirals from the end of the corridor became more and more numerous, it did not affect Luo Zheng's speed.
As for the strange situation in the desert on both sides of the corridor, Luo Zheng was too lazy to observe it one by one. What he saw each time was almost the same.
After moving forward like this for another hour, Luo Zheng suddenly heard the whistling sound of a flute.
Along with the whistling sound of the flute, a fine grain of colorful sand came from the end of the corridor and brushed past Luo Zheng's face. It was as if someone had cut his face with a knife.

Fusion
"Dong dong dong..."

After 9527 finished speaking, a light green soul suddenly drilled out of Luo Zheng's chest.
This soul was also a split of 9527, or a very small part of 9527 himself. But this part of the soul contained some of 9527's memories.
"Dong dong dong..."
The baby was only a few hundred feet away from Luo Zheng. As a newborn will, he did not have a physical body. Even if he turned into the shape of a baby, it was not a real body.
In this form, the baby was very sensitive to the power of the soul. The green soul quickly attracted his attention.
"Whoosh!"
Under the control of 9527, the split of the soul was very cooperative. After circling in the air, it hit the baby's mouth. The split of the soul was swallowed by the baby just like that.
The baby smacked his lips and looked at Luo Zheng as if he had tasted something delicious. He knew that Luo Zheng should have something delicious like this.
But just as he stepped out, his huge body suddenly trembled. A wisp of green gradually appeared in his originally pure eyes, and his eyes gradually became dull. He actually walked unsteadily towards Luo Zheng.
At this moment, Luo Zheng also found that his body could move. He asked curiously, "What did you do?"
It seemed that 9527 had tamed the baby.
"It's very simple. He swallowed the split soul, and the newborn will was contaminated by me. There was only one will in the split soul, which was to become Luo Zheng's food, so he also thought he was your food," 9527 said lightly.
Hearing such an answer, Luo Zheng was speechless. The newborn will of the Domain of the Gods was so easily tamed?
9527 also knew what Luo Zheng was thinking, and explained disdainfully, "I told you, you can just let the newborn will swallow it, and you will still be the one to survive in the end. I just used a different method, and the result is the same."
Luo Zheng pursed his lips. Despite what 9527 said, he would not allow himself to be devoured by this guy.
As the baby got closer and closer, his body began to shrink rapidly. Finally, he became palm-sized and flew towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng still had a vigilant look on his face. When the baby got into Luo Zheng's mind and showed no intention of attacking, he was sure that the newborn will was indeed for him to swallow. Without any hesitation, he absorbed it into his soul.
It was very difficult for two different souls to merge. Even if it did, it would cause a lot of problems. For example, possession would mean expelling the other soul, or directly crushing it into pieces. This was the saying that two tigers could not live on the same mountain.
But in the newborn will, there was no other thought except for 9527's split soul. It was very pure, and Luo Zheng had no difficulty in swallowing it.
However, swallowing the newborn will of the Domain of the Gods was not something that could be done in an instant. After all, the newborn will of the Domain of the Gods was born from the entire Domain of the Gods, and its own will was still very large.
After a full four hours, a cross-shaped pattern quietly appeared between Luo Zheng's eyebrows. This cross-shaped pattern appeared in a flash, and then quietly disappeared into his forehead.
"Hum …"
As Luo Zheng opened his eyes, the Saint Mountain, which was originally floating steadily in the air, suddenly shook.
Luo Zheng looked at his hands, and then looked at the world around him. Suddenly, he felt that everything was a little different. His understanding of the entire Domain of the Gods seemed to have crossed a level. Some knowledge and abilities that did not belong to him originally appeared out of thin air in his body.
This feeling of having everything in his mind was very strange, and even made him a little unaccustomed.
He looked down, and with just a thought, the colorful desert suddenly became active. The grains of sand quickly arranged themselves, like closely arranged soldiers.
The desert disappeared, and in its place was a colorful staircase.
Luo Zheng followed the staircase and slowly walked toward the passageway. The passageway in the maze was originally crooked, and it was impossible to walk out without the guidance of the Immeasurable Ruler. However, all the passageways were changing their direction and shape. What appeared in front of Luo Zheng was a straight path.

The competition between the Quasi-sages and the Perfect True Gods had reached a white-hot stage.

It was not that they did not want to continue challenging the Holy Springs, but at this stage of the competition, everyone was already an arrow at the end of its flight. Whoever rushed into the Holy Springs would be ambushed by the other party. Once the ambush succeeded, it would be fatal. Therefore, those Quasi-sages who were powerful simply stood aside and watched.
They were stuffing supreme-grade elixirs into their mouths as if they were free. They all hoped to recover to their best state in the shortest time possible.
At this moment, Luo Zheng had already swept across the plain and was heading straight for the Holy Springs.
His speed seemed very slow, and he was just floating leisurely. However, his absolute speed was actually astonishingly fast.
The scale of space seemed to have lost its effect on him. The distance of one Zhang that he moved forward was the distance of one Zhang that others moved. To create this effect, one could only use the Greater Teleportation. However, Luo Zheng obviously did not use it. There was not even a fluctuation of space around him.
