After Pu Zhe's entire body crashed into the mountain range, a huge crater appeared below. Pu Zhe was still alive, and he actually slowly crawled out of the crater.
"Pah!"
Pu Zhe spat out a mouthful of blood, which transformed into a blood arrow that punched a bloody hole in the solid layer of rock underground.
There was no trace of fear on his face. Instead, a raging desire to fight surged within him. A person like him was born to fight, and the more powerful the opponent, the more his desire to fight would be aroused.
"Interesting. There's no one in the Realm of the Gods who can fight. Hahaha, I want to fight you for three hundred rounds!" Pu Zhe laughed.
Strange patterns surged all over his body, and his body size increased explosively.
Luo Zheng looked down at Pu Zhe and shook his head indifferently. "I don't have time to play with you …"
As he spoke, he tapped his finger lightly, and threads of lightning blossomed from his fingertip, transforming into a small silver dragon. As Luo Zheng flicked his finger downwards, the small silver dragon burst out with dazzling light, transforming into a massive lightning punishment that poured down on Pu Zhe.

Chapter 2582
A Desperate Struggle
The Lightning Punishment gradually receded, transforming into a small lightning dragon that returned to Luo Zheng's side.
Pu Zhe's body had already been reduced to ashes …
The originally massive crater had deepened by several thousand feet under the bombardment of the Lightning Punishment, and a green stream of Black Flame gushed out of the crater.
Shi Qiaoqiao and Sage Jiu couldn't help but gasp at this sight.
Luo Zheng's gaze didn't linger as he looked at Uncle Hou on the other side.
Even now, Uncle Hou's mind was somewhat muddled. How had Luo Zheng become so powerful in such a short amount of time? What exactly was that small lightning dragon with astonishing power?
He couldn't think of an answer to these questions, but under Luo Zheng's gaze, he understood one thing: if he didn't leave now, he would die here.
The moment Uncle Hou used Greater Teleportation, Luo Zheng's eyes blinked.
An invisible ripple spread out with him as the center. Houbo immediately discovered that his power had been sealed, and his entire body plummeted downwards.
Shi Qiaoqiao's eyes flashed at this sight.
She was very familiar with this power. This was the power of the Silencing Scepter, and Luo Zheng could use it without using the Silencing Scepter!
Luo Zheng took a step forward, and before his figure had completely disappeared, he had already appeared on the other side.
At this moment, his arm rapidly swelled up. His Origin Energy flowed downwards, and he had already gathered an astonishing amount of power.
This power far surpassed Yu Taibai's fist!
"Whoosh …"
He threw a punch forward. Then, Bo Ze fell down and collided with his fist.
"Bo!"
This punch didn't explode with any violent movements, but with a muffled sound, Uncle Hou's body was pulverized by Luo Zheng.
Before the scattered flesh and blood splashed onto Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng's figure slowly dissipated, and he reappeared at Shi Qiaoqiao and Sage Jiu's side.
"Let's go," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
"Where to?" Shi Qiaoqiao asked.
"Saint Mountain. There are still a few people in the Divine Realm that need to be eliminated," Luo Zheng replied.
Shi Qiaoqiao and Saint Jiu smiled, naturally understanding.
Luo Zheng tapped lightly with his finger and teleported Saint Jiu and Shi Qiaoqiao towards Saint Mountain.
Regardless of the distance, Luo Zheng only needed one step to travel through God's Domain.
From the time Luo Zheng left the Saint Mountain to the time he returned with Shi Xiaoqiao and Saint Jiu, only a few dozen breaths had passed.
The Sages did not know what had happened, but Pang Miao's face darkened even more. "Where are Puzhe and Uncle Hou?"
"It's already gone," Luo Zheng said.
When the Sages heard this, their faces were filled with shock.
The Xuan Yuan Guard had no natural enemies in Divine Domain. They were invincible existences. Even the weakest Xuan Yuan Guard, Hu-yan, could crush everyone!
In such a short time, Luo Zheng had killed two Xuanyuan Guards?
Luo Zheng's strength rose to another level in the hearts of the Sages.
All sorts of thoughts ran through the Sages' minds. Most of them were actually somewhat happy.
These Sages did not like Luo Zheng, but they hated Emperor Hanqing even more. Emperor Hanqing had gone against the tide with the support of the Sages. These Sages were filled with resentment.
If Luo Zheng had such heaven-defying strength, Emperor Hanqing's fate could be easily imagined.
Compared to the other Sages, Emperor Hanqing was not as excited. He had already fallen into an ice cellar. Once Pang Miao and the others failed, Emperor Hanqing could easily imagine his fate.
Pang Miao narrowed his eyes and sighed, "Just one wrong move …. It's really hard to believe that this would happen."
"I can give you time to end your own lives," Luo Zheng said softly.
Pang Miao's face brimmed with a confident smile, "The Xuan Yuan Guard has never been able to compromise. Not in the past, not in the future!"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a faint light, "In the future …. In the future …. I will wipe out all of the Xuan Yuan Guard, including the Yellow Emperor Xuanyuan!"
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Pang Miao, Huyan, and Cha Yao all laughed.
"Hahaha, you think you can dominate the chaos just because you've merged with a newborn intent? A brat who hasn't even stepped into the Nirvana Realm wants to wipe out our Xuan Yuan Guard? What a joke! "Pang Miao laughed maniacally.
Luo Zheng's strength was not his own. He borrowed the power of the Divine Domain.
Now, he was just like in his internal world. He was almost undefeatable, but that was only within the Divine Domain.
Once he left the Divine Domain, he would still be a Perfect True God. Of course, his true strength was much stronger than normal Perfect True Gods.
However, the current Luo Zheng had difficulty even walking in the Mother World. Now that he wanted to wipe out the Xuan Yuan Guard, the Xuan Yuan Guard naturally thought he was spouting nonsense.
In the face of Pang Miao's mockery, Luo Zheng's expression did not change at all.
It was true that he could not do it now, but he had never stopped in the Eastern Region, in the universe, or in the Divine Dominion. Naturally, he would not stop in the future. Controlling the Divine Dominion was only the first step in his journey.
"Things are always changing …" Luo Zheng smiled faintly. "Just like how you never expected that a saint born in the universe who was once Dongfang Chunjun's enemy would be able to personally end your lives."
"The Mother World is different from what you think. The Li Clan that your mother represents has already been completely destroyed. That place is not the Divine Domain. Even if you go there, you'll be nothing more than a rootless duckweed. What do you have to fight us?" Pang Miao said proudly.
The moment the You Xiong Clan joined the Xuan Yuan Guard, they treated it as their life's glory.
Luo Zheng did not want to argue with Pang Miao. He only shook his head. "It's a pity you won't live to see that day." As he spoke, Luo Zheng flicked his finger. The small lightning dragon circling around his finger shot out.
Seeing that Luo Zheng had already made his move, Pang Miao, Huyan, and Uncle Hou also exploded with shocking auras!
"Kekeke …"
The Sea Splitter Tooth's enormous mouth opened and closed. Instantly, blood-red light soared into the sky.
Although Pang Miao was arguing with Luo Zheng, he was also calculating the various possibilities of defeating Luo Zheng.
Although the power of the lightning tribulation was under Luo Zheng's control, if Pang Miao used his full strength, he would still be able to withstand it.
"I'll trap this lightning tribulation!" Pang Miao roared. Instantly, blood-red shadows charged out of the Sea Splitter Tooth. These blood-red shadows each took a position, forming a formation to trap the lightning tribulation within.
Huyan and Cha Yao's figures flickered, appearing at Luo Zheng's side.
Even Emperor Hanqing gripped his Divine Taihe Spear and charged forward. Although he knew that he was no match for Luo Zheng, he had no choice but to fight to the death.
"Outside the Divine Domain, my strength might not be worth mentioning, but within the Divine Domain, you will never understand …"

Chapter 2583
Extinguishing the Flames of Memory
Hu-yan's eyes shot out blood-red flames towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng didn't dodge and allowed the flames to burn him, but he wasn't hurt at all.
The silver light on the surface of the Saint Mountain behind him was rippling …
This indestructible Saint's Mountain could help Luo Zheng deal almost any damage!
On the other side, it was the same for Cha Yao. Silver threads were hanging from his hand. These threads were extremely sharp and almost indestructible, but Luo Zheng remained unmoved despite being entangled by these threads.
In the divine territory, these Nirvana Realm experts couldn't hurt Luo Zheng, unless they had enough power to fight against the entire divine territory!
Luo Zheng's thoughts moved slightly. Buzhou Spirit Mountain's power gathered in his body and flowed quickly through his veins. The veins on his right arm bulged again as power gathered crazily.
At the same time, Luo Zheng's finger tapped lightly. An invisible power spread out and enveloped Cha Yao and Hu-yan.
Even though Shi Xiaoqiao controlled Soul Desolation, she couldn't compare to Luo Zheng in terms of using the Silencing Scepter.
Every time she used the Silencing Scepter, she could only choose to silence the entire divine territory. But how could Luo Zheng control the size and range of this power at will?
Similarly, Buzhou Spirit Mountain was the same. Luo Zheng could draw more power from Buzhou Spirit Mountain than Yu Taibai.
After Cha Yao and Hu-yan were silenced, the flames and silver threads enveloping them disappeared without a trace. They couldn't even fly and fell rapidly downwards.
Luo Zheng's figure flashed and appeared beside Hu-yan. His huge fist crushed space and smashed onto Hu-yan's body.
"Dong …"
Hu-yan's body caved in. Under the huge power, the caved in deeper and deeper. His body began to disintegrate and quickly turned into a bloody mist.
As Luo Zheng attacked Hu-yan, a black ball formed in his left hand. This was the power of the Abyss Demon Realm!
He gently pushed the black sphere, which broke through space and enveloped Cha Yao's body.
If Cha Yao hadn't been silenced by Luo Zheng, he might have had the ability to resist this black sphere. However, Cha Yao couldn't even fly now. How could he use other mystiques? They could only stay in the darkness and be slowly annihilated by the black energy.
With the help of the Taihe Divine Spear, Emperor Hanqing appeared above Luo Zheng without a sound.
Even someone like Hu Yan would have a hard time detecting such a ghostly teleportation technique.
But what in the God's Domain could escape Luo Zheng's eyes and ears?
The moment Emperor Han Qing appeared, Luo Zheng raised his head slightly and glanced at Emperor Han Qing.
Emperor Hanqing felt a chill run down his spine. His hands trembled, but he gritted his teeth and continued to wield the Taihe Divine Spear.
"Let's fight!"
The Taihe Divine Spear released powerful spatial laws, forming a turbulent spatial spiral that shot towards Luo Zheng's face.
If this spatial spiral hit another saint, it would probably pierce a hole in their body. If it hit their head, even their soul would be destroyed.
Luo Zheng still raised his head. He had no intention of dodging. The spatial spiral crazily tore at Luo Zheng's face, but he didn't react at all. This power couldn't harm him at all.
On the side of Saint Mountain, spirals appeared in the silver light. All of Emperor Han Qing's mystiques were here.
"It's easy to kill the Xuan Yuan Guard. Killing you … is even easier!" Luo Zheng said expressionlessly. He raised his right hand as he spoke.
Though Luo Zheng didn't release the slightest trace of dao, Emperor Han Qing felt a chill run down his spine. Almost like a conditioned reflex, he frantically retreated, pulling away from Luo Zheng.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng smiled faintly and grabbed at a certain direction in the sky.
Rumble rumble rumble rumble …
From the depths of the God's Domain came a series of explosions. This was the sound of the universe collapsing.
Emperor Han Qing's face fell, his body trembling. "You, you, you destroyed my universe?" he roared.
Luo Zheng nodded expressionlessly. "Someone like you shouldn't have been born in the God's Domain. It's best if you die!"
"You!" Emperor Han Qing's eyes bulged as if he wanted to swallow Luo Zheng whole.
Rumble rumble rumble …
As the universe continued to collapse, so did Emperor Han Qing's dantian.
If any other true god had their dantian shattered, they'd at most become a cripple. But a saint was different.
When a saint created a universe, they connected themselves to it. If the universe didn't collapse, the saint wouldn't die. But if the universe collapsed, the saint would fall with it.
That was why the people of the mother world were not very interested in the 'Soul Lodging Grounds'. While being immortal and indestructible was a great advantage, its weakness was equally obvious. Once one tied their fate to the Soul Lodging Grounds, the Soul Lodging Grounds would become their weakness.
Crack crack crack …
Thousands of cracks appeared on Emperor Han Qing's dantian. They extended from his dantian to his body, stomach, legs, chest, neck, and finally to his head. Even his soul!
Before he died, Emperor Han Qing didn't speak. Instead, he glared at Luo Zheng with hatred.
Luo Zheng looked him in the eye. "You have a part of the Stone Forest Rat's abilities, and you've even hidden your memories. You think … I don't know?"
Emperor Han Qing's cracked face twitched, and his eyes revealed a trace of fear.
"You took your memories away from your place of reincarnation and hid them in the tomb of your Yue Clan's Ancestral Land. You've left yourself a way out," Luo Zheng said as he made a grabbing motion.
An invisible ripple of space passed through half of the Divine Realm and headed straight for a Divine City in the Ten Thousand Willows Region.
The Yue Clan used to be the largest clan in the Ten Thousand Willows Region. They were once a Noble Clan and had even been on the Floating Island …
After Yue Yinliu's death, the Ten Thousand Willows Region became desolate. Even the Yue Clan's Ancestral Land's Divine City was abandoned.
The Noble Alliance had once banned the Yue Clan. Even though the Yue Clan's Ancestral Land had a Thousand Commandments Slate, no other clan dared to occupy the Divine City. Some people who passed by the Yue Clan's Divine City would sigh. It was such a waste that such a majestic Divine City was abandoned.
At this moment, in a desolate mountain range behind the Divine City, a tomb covered in moss suddenly trembled.
"Bo!"

Chapter 2584
The Token of Paramita
In the blink of an eye, the box had traveled two hundred billion kilometers to appear in Luo Zheng's hands.
"Crack …"
The box cracked open, revealing the Flame of Memories within.
Luo Zheng had acquired the reincarnation soul armament in the Land of Reincarnation, but he hadn't been able to find Emperor Hanqing's memories. Luo Zheng knew that Emperor Hanqing must've done something to hide the Flame of Memories, but Luo Zheng didn't know where.
However, he was now in an omniscient state regarding the God's Domain. Luo Zheng only had a slight doubt in his heart, and he already knew the entire truth.
"I'm not Dongfang Chunjun," Luo Zheng whispered. "I won't give you a chance to make a comeback. Let your memories die with you …"
Emperor Hanqing's face contorted with fear when he saw the box.
He didn't fear the collapse of the universe, nor did he fear the destruction of his body. As long as the Flame of Memories existed, he could make a comeback.
When he'd saved the Flame of Memories, he knew he was invincible.
His talent was extraordinary, and he had the experience of two lives as a Saint. Even if he died, he could be reborn again and again!
Emperor Hanqing never imagined that Luo Zheng would find his Flame of Memories so easily!
"You, how did you know!?" Emperor Hanqing's voice trembled, the hatred in his eyes vanishing.
"The realm of Gods has no secrets to me," Luo Zheng replied.
"Let me go, I beg you, please give me a chance. My two daughters, I can let them accompany you, let them serve you …" Emperor Hanqing begged.
It would have been fine if Emperor Han Qing did not mention Han Liusu and Han Chuyue, but when he did, Luo Zheng's heart was suddenly filled with anger. With a light twist of his finger, the green flame of memories was extinguished.
The universe collapsed, his soul was destroyed, and even the Flame of Memories was extinguished. Emperor Hanqing was truly dead.
With so many layers of protection, the average person wouldn't be able to kill him. Even the Xuan Yuan Wei would find it difficult to do so.
Seeing the fate of Emperor Hanqing, the Sages could not help but shudder.
In fact, the death of Emperor Hanqing was exactly what the Sages were looking forward to. Even Tang Lun and Mu Haiji, who had a grudge with Luo Zheng, were the same.
However, the fact that Luo Zheng could shatter a part of the universe with a casual wave of his hand still made the Sages unable to remain calm. This meant that the fate of all the Sages was under Luo Zheng's control. Whoever he wanted to die, they had no choice but to die!
"Crackle …"
Not far away, Pang Miao was still struggling against the lightning tribulation.
Pang Miao's ability was indeed beyond Luo Zheng's expectations. Pang Miao seemed to have used up all the blood in the Sea Rending Tooth. His whole body was dripping with blood. This blood seemed to be able to pollute the Thunder Punishment.
Every time that bolt of lightning struck his body, the power of the lightning would dim a little. Not long after, Pang Miao finally dispelled the lightning punishment.
However, Pang Miao was currently in an extremely sorry state. His entire body was charred, and his aura was waning. Moreover, his face was ghastly pale.
The Sea Rending Tooth was also thrown to the ground. The surface of the fish bone was dried up and covered with cracks.
"Withstanding a lightning punishment is indeed not bad. Unfortunately, your trump card seems to have been destroyed," Luo Zheng said.
Without the help of the Sea Rending Tooth, Pang Miao would not be able to break through Shi Xiaoqiao's Silencing Scepter. Furthermore, Luo Zheng could use the power of the Soul Desolate without any restrictions. In addition to Pang Miao's current state, he was simply unable to fight against Luo Zheng.
Pang Miao's face was gloomy as he glared at Luo Zheng.
In less than two hours, the four Xuan Yuan Guards who had followed him for so many years had fallen. One could imagine the unwillingness in his heart.
"What a pity. If Lord Wei Xian had entered the Divine Realm earlier, he would have been able to stop you from entering the Saint Mountain …" Pang Miao muttered.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was slightly stunned. "Lord Wei Xian? Who is that? "
Seeing Luo Zheng's reaction, Pang Miao felt a trace of joy in his heart. "That's someone from our You Xiong Clan, an expert who joined the Heavensguard Pavilion. He should have slaughtered all those fellows from the Outer Realm by now, right? Hahaha! "
Luo Zheng's expression suddenly changed.
He knew everything that was happening in the Divine Realm, but he knew nothing about what was happening outside the Divine Realm.
His mother and father were in the Outer Realm, but with Elder Jin and Ming Wei's protection, Luo Zheng was certain that they were absolutely safe. However, listening to Pang Miao's words, there were actually other Xuan Yuan Guards who had come to the Divine Realm?
Just as Luo Zheng was distracted, a trace of madness appeared in Pang Miao's eyes.
His figure suddenly flashed, and he was already very close to Luo Zheng.
"Ka, ka, ka …"
Pang Miao's entire body was plastered to Luo Zheng's back, and his hands tightly gripped Luo Zheng's ribs.
"You can kill me if you want, but you'll have to pay the price with your life. Hahahaha …"
Traces of blood-colored threads gushed out from Pang Miao's body, and these soft blood threads immediately bound him and Luo Zheng together. An indescribable aura erupted from Pang Miao's body.
After all, Luo Zheng was still too young. After being distracted by Pang Miao, he actually let Pang Miao attack him.
With a thought, he used the power of the Soul Wilderness, wanting to confine the power in Pang Miao's body.
However, the power of the Soul Wilderness was useless at this moment, and it could not suppress that aura!
"Luo Zheng, he's burning the Paramita Token," 9527 reminded him.
"Paramita Token?"
Luo Zheng did not know what it was.
"It's the source of power for Nirvana Realm powerhouses. It's also the most important thing for Nirvana Realm powerhouses. Burning the Nirvana Token will result in the punishment of the truth. His ending will also be very miserable …" 9527 explained.
Pang Miao's eyes had already turned dark red, and his entire body seemed to be soaked in blood. The power that erupted from him had completely exceeded his own strength.
The power in his body began to expand wildly, and Pang Miao, who was originally slightly thin, actually turned into a big fatty at this moment …
When the Saints nearby felt Pang Miao's power, they were all shocked, and they all used the Great Shift to get away.
"Luo Zheng!"
"Luo Zheng!"
Not far away, Saint Jiu and Shi Qiaoqiao wanted to rush over.
"Go away!"
Luo Zheng warned.
He also could not estimate how much power Pang Miao could unleash by burning the Paramita Token. After all, he did not even know what the Paramita Token was.
Under the circumstances, Luo Zheng's mind moved, and he directly used the Great Shift. He actually brought the wildly expanding Pang Miao into the spatial passage, and teleported to the depths of the Sea of Time.
Shi Qiaoqiao and Saint Jiu watched as Luo Zheng disappeared with Pang Miao, and the next moment, a dull roar erupted in the Sea of Time. The ground around the Sea of Time was constantly shaking, and bright red blood appeared in the center of the Sea of Time.

Chapter 2585
Translator: Nyoi-Bo Studio Editor: Nyoi-Bo Studio
Goodbye Maha
In the depths of the Sea of Time, Luo Zheng floated slowly.
Scarlet blood flowed in the fragments of the Sea of Time. This blood was like oil that did not dissolve in water and would not be swallowed by the Sea of Time. It was quite strange.
The moment Pang Miao self-destructed, Luo Zheng saw a pale red triangular fragment. It was that triangular fragment that was burning fiercely.
"That triangular fragment is the Nirvana token?" Luo Zheng asked.
When that triangular fragment appeared, Luo Zheng felt a completely different aura. He had never seen such an aura before.
"Yes," 9527 replied. "The power of all Nirvana Realm masters comes from a token. You'll know when you really set foot on the Nirvana Realm."
By burning the Nirvana token to self-destruct, the power was indeed unimaginable. However, it was naive of Pang Miao to want to drag Luo Zheng to his death.
If one wanted to kill Luo Zheng in the Divine Realm, one had to have the ability to destroy the entire Divine Realm. At present, Luo Zheng had not met such an opponent.
Luo Zheng thought quietly for a moment. Suddenly, he remembered that Pang Miao had mentioned something about Tianshou Pavilion and Lord Wei Xian.
"Oh no, outside the Divine Realm …"
Luo Zheng could not tell whether Pang Miao was telling the truth or not, but he was obviously very confident in this Lord Wei Xian.
If Elder Jin and Ming Wei could not handle it, wouldn't the others be in danger?
Those Saints were still fine. Even if they were killed, they could be reborn in the universe. However, his mother did not have a place to rest her soul …
"Hum …"
Luo Zheng's figure trembled slightly. He had disappeared from the bottom of the Sea of Time and appeared in front of the Saint Mountain in an instant.
Shi Qiaoqiao and Elder Jin felt the vibration on the other side of the Sea of Time. They were still worried. When they saw that Luo Zheng had returned safely, they were relieved.
"I want to investigate the situation outside. I'll leave this to you," Luo Zheng said faintly. He glanced at the Saints in the distance.
Saint Jiu frowned slightly. "But your power …"
"I won't leave the Domain of the Gods," Luo Zheng said.
Luo Zheng's current cultivation level was still that of a Great Perfection True God. In terms of ability, he was slightly stronger than a Saint. All his reliance was within the Divine Territory. If he really left the Divine Territory, he wouldn't be able to fight against a Nirvana Realm expert.
"It's best to be careful," Shi Xiaoqiao advised.
Luo Zheng nodded. His figure dissipated again, and in the next moment, he appeared at the top of a universe. This was Mu Hai Ji's Ultimate Universe.
The location Ming Wei had chosen was right above the Great Pole Space, so Dongfang Chunjun had gone through Mu Haiji's Great Pole Space to find out where Ming Wei and the others were.
"A green star?"
Luo Zheng's eyes flickered when he saw the lonely star at the top of the Great Pole Universe.
Right at this moment, another four or five streaks of light flew over from afar. The living beings in the Great Extremity Universe should be the "Fake Saint Clan" that invaded the Great Evolution Universe back then.
"Who dares to wander around the forbidden zone of the universe?!"
These people were still some distance away from Luo Zheng, but an arrogant voice suddenly roared out.
Luo Zheng didn't see the appearance of the newcomer, but this voice sounded familiar. He seemed to have heard this voice somewhere before.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh …
The distance between them and Luo Zheng quickly shrank. Luo Zheng looked over and saw the newcomer's appearance. Only then did he remember the other party's identity.
"Holy Son Maha?" Luo Zheng glanced at the other party and said.
Maha was slightly surprised to hear his name being called out. His eyes focused on Luo Zheng's face for a moment, and then a sharp light suddenly shot out of his eyes. "You are … Luo Zheng!"
Back in the Sunflower Water Realm, Maha had single-handedly challenged all the geniuses of the universe, shaking the entire Great Evolution Universe. Even Xuanyuan Chenfeng had been defeated by him. In the end, Luo Zheng had killed Maha.
But Maha was the Holy Son. The "fake Holy Race" had already prepared for both sides. After returning to the Great Evolution Universe, Maha had once again obtained a powerful treasure body.
But after Maha was resurrected, the fake Holy Race had already returned in defeat …
As for what happened in the Great Evolution Universe, Maha didn't know much.
The Great Evolution Universe war had ended with the defeat of the Great Evolution Universe. Many Empyreans of the Great Evolution Universe had fallen, and there were many vacancies in the Empyrean positions.
However, Maha still didn't inherit an Empyrean position. He still painstakingly cultivated, hoping that one day he could rush out of the universe and step into the Realm of the Gods.
During this time, the Great Evolution Universe wasn't peaceful. After all, there was a group of Nirvana Realm powerhouses fighting at the peak of the Great Evolution Universe. Although the Heavenly Mirror Shield isolated everything, the movements of the Nirvana Realm powerhouses fighting were far too loud. Thunderous sounds would often pass down.
Maha brought people to patrol the area. Coincidentally, they ran into Luo Zheng who was passing by.
"It's me." Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed.
He didn't have a good impression of this Maha. Although the Great Evolution Universe war had been started by Mu Haiji and the fake Holy Race's secondary life forms were nothing more than his chess pieces, this person had still led the fake Holy Race to stir up trouble in the Great Evolution Universe.
But so much time had passed. Luo Zheng looked back and saw that some things had become indifferent. In front of him, Maha was infinitely small and insignificant.
But Maha didn't think so. When he had been killed by Luo Zheng, it had been a great humiliation. Time passed quickly in the Great Evolution Universe, so only two or three hundred years had passed. But this debt was firmly engraved in his heart.
Woosh!
Maha suddenly thrust out a spear, a cold smile on his face. "I don't know what your goal is in sneaking into the Great Evolution Universe, but since you've come, don't even think of leaving."
Luo Zheng was now a Perfect True Divinity, and he had restrained his aura.
In the Great Evolution Universe, even an Empyrean could only distinguish between a lower True Divinity and a middle True Divinity. They simply couldn't see through Luo Zheng's cultivation.
But Maha thought that Luo Zheng had used some secret technique to conceal his cultivation and was only a mere World Lord.
Moreover, the Great Evolution Universe was his home ground! If they fought in the Great Evolution Universe, the Saint Mu Haiji could appear at any time. No matter how strong Luo Zheng was, could he be stronger than the Saint who had created the Great Evolution Universe?
Thus, Maha had a confident expression on his face.
"It's been two hundred years, but your personality is still the same …" Luo Zheng shook his head.
Back in the Great Evolution Universe, Luo Zheng had been a proud son of heaven. But after stepping into the Realm of the Gods, he realized just how insignificant he was.
Maha's father was a Great Empyrean, so he could naturally throw his weight around in the Great Evolution Universe. If he still had this personality after stepping into the Realm of the Gods, he would likely suffer a great loss.
Before Luo Zheng could finish speaking, Maha flicked the spear in his hands. The spear stabbed towards Luo Zheng like a poisonous dragon.
But this spear was only able to pierce halfway before it was unable to advance any further. An invisible strength was preventing the spear from moving any further.

Chapter 2586
Trapping Array
As the master of the Great Hall, Mu Haiji knew everything in the universe like the back of his hand.
The moment Luo Zheng entered the Great Hall, Mu Haiji had already known about it.
But Mu Haiji didn't say anything. Luo Zheng's current strength was not something he could provoke. He knew when to advance and when to retreat, so he naturally didn't dare to offend Luo Zheng.
But even Mu Haiji didn't expect that Luo Zheng would encounter Maha in the Great Hall. Moreover, Maha had dared to attack Luo Zheng without saying a word!
He didn't care about Maha's life or death, but his fate was in Luo Zheng's hands. If Maha offended Luo Zheng, then there would be great trouble!
"Extreme … Extreme Saint!"
Seeing Mu Haiji suddenly appear, Maha began to stammer.
The faces of the people following behind Maha also changed greatly.
The Extreme Saint was the master of this universe, and this world was created by him. Mu Haiji was the source of all things in the Great Hall! Even the most powerful Grand Empyreans could be casually crushed by the Extreme Saint.
The vast majority of people in the Great Hall didn't know of the existence of the Extreme Saint. A small number of people would be filled with awe when the Extreme Saint was mentioned.

Hearing this, a chill ran down Mu Haiji's spine. He quickly said, "No, no, is the entire Realm of the Gods a place for you to go? However, there are still some people in this universe who don't have eyes. If you come here personally, of course, I'll clean up everything for you … "
Luo Zheng faintly glanced at Mu Haiji. In any case, this guy wouldn't be able to run away. The debt between his father and Mu Haiji would be settled by his father. He coldly said, "I won't bother with him."

There was an invisible barrier beneath the green planet at the top of the Divine Realm. This barrier prevented all living things from passing through. Only Mu Haiji could pass through.
Seeing Luo Zheng continue to move upwards, Mu Haiji hurriedly removed the invisible barrier.
When Maha heard the conversation between Mu Haiji and Luo Zheng, he felt as if he had been struck by lightning. His entire body stiffened as he floated in the air.
He couldn't believe his ears!
This was the Supreme Saint! It was a Saint! The Supreme Saint was one of the most powerful existences in the Divine Realm!
And that Luo Zheng was merely a Sector Lord 300 years ago!
No matter how high Luo Zheng's talent was, how long had it been since he entered the Divine Realm? The flow of time in the Divine Realm was much slower than in the universe. Even though Luo Zheng had entered the Divine Realm right after the Universal War ended, he had only been there for 20-30 years.
20-30 years and he was on the same level as a Saint?
It wasn't just on the same level!

That was the face of a weak person facing a strong person!
This guy …
Maha was the Saint Son. In the entire universe, no one dared to provoke him. Indeed, no one was stronger than him in terms of talent.
It was just that his father had spent 200 years transforming his treasure body. But in time, he would definitely step into the Divine Realm.
Losing the Universal War and being killed by Luo Zheng was the greatest stain in his life. He had made countless vows in his heart that one day, he would take revenge.
So when he saw Luo Zheng again, his heart was filled with hatred!
But after seeing Mu Haiji's attitude, Maha understood the difference between him and Luo Zheng.
He completely believed that even if Luo Zheng killed him in front of Mu Haiji, Mu Haiji wouldn't dare say a word.
And the reason why Luo Zheng didn't do anything was because he was as light as a speck of dust in front of him …
Not killing him was the greatest contempt. Maha felt like his mind was about to explode.

This stone tablet had considerable authority in the divine realm. In terms of influence, it was probably second only to Luo Zheng, who had fused with the nascent will.
Since Luo Zheng had written down the order to kill the Xuan Yuan Guards, the Governing Stone Tablet naturally knew where they were hiding.
"Alright, the Governing Slate will kill them." Luo Zheng smiled at Elder Jin and Ming Wei.
Ming Wei nodded. "The power of this stone slab is indeed very strong. However, with just one bolt of lightning, it's more than enough to kill the other Xuan Yuan Guards. However, I'm afraid that it'll be difficult for Jing Xian."
Before this, Ming Wei had transformed into her Aragami form and unleashed all of her power. However, she still could not kill Wei Xian.
This Wei Xian probably had a unique blood refinement technique, so he wasn't that easy to kill.
"If one thunderbolt isn't enough, then ten, a hundred, a thousand, ten thousand. Once the rules of the Governing Slate are triggered, they won't stop until they die. There's no way they can escape," Luo Zheng replied.
Luo Zheng had only been able to avoid the punishment of the Governing Slate's rules in the Great Yan Universe through the method of blood exchange.
If one were to use other methods to sneak into the Divine Territory, the moment they violated the rules of the stone slab, they would face an endless stream of punishment.
Hearing Luo Zheng's explanation, Ming Wei smiled and nodded in understanding.
Luo Zheng's gaze finally landed on Fu Er. Fu Er had been unconscious all this time. After his legs had been cut off by Wei Xian, Chi Yi had been carrying him on his back.
"How are Fu Er's injuries?" Luo Zheng asked with a frown.
Chi Yi said, "They're not fatal. He'll just need to rest."
Luo Zheng nodded. "Then I'll naturally find a quiet place for everyone!" His gaze swept across the floating islands below. "Let's go to the Han Clan's floating island first."
The Luo Clan's floating island had already fallen into the Sea of Time. However, to the Luo Clan, having another floating island was only a matter of a thought from Luo Zheng. There was no rush. After settling the internal and external troubles in the Divine Territory, there were still many things to take care of.
…
…
Following the fall of the Han Clan's Sage Emperor, the entire clan was without a leader. Even Han Tianxiao was still fighting for the position of Sage in Saint Mountain. There were only a few Han Clan elders guarding the floating island.
Many of the Han Clan's disciples were afraid of being implicated, so they all fled the floating island and returned to the Liuren Domain.
The arrival of Luo Zheng's group caused the Han Clan elders to shiver in fear. During the time Emperor Han Qing had commanded the Noble Clan Alliance, all of the Han Clan's disciples had been bossy and domineering.
Now that Emperor Han Qing had fallen, the entire Han Clan was finished. Many of the Han Clan's disciples believed so.
Chi Yi, Elder Jin, and the others found a secluded place to treat Fu Er's injuries. Ming Wei was focused on repairing her severed arm.
In comparison, Luo Xiao and Li Luoshui were in the best condition out of the group of outsiders.
Under the gazes of the elders of the Han family, Luo Xiao gently spread his hands. With a spatial ripple, a spatial tunnel was constructed. The tunnel directly connected to a certain star in the Grand Yan Universe.
Soon …
Aunt Han Jiu, Jing Wuhuan, Yu Taibai, Han Chuyue, Han Liusu, and the others walked out.
"Aunt Han Jiu!"
"It's a quasi-sage!"
"And Princess Liusu …"
When the elders of the Han Family saw these people, they couldn't contain their excitement.
Even an idiot could see that the Sea of Time was about to change.
If Aunt Han Jiu endorsed the Han Clan, the entire Han Clan would be able to survive this calamity unscathed!
"Huh? Why is it the Han Clan's floating island?" Han Chuyue blinked, her face full of curiosity.
Han Liusu was also a bit confused, her large eyes staring blankly at Luo Zheng.
"I couldn't find a safe place for now, so I could only choose this place," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
Yu Taibai, Aunt Han Jiu, and the others saw that Luo Zheng was safe and sound. They roughly guessed the general situation of the Divine Realm. During this period of time, they had been hiding in the Great Yan Universe and bitterly waiting.
Just as they were about to ask about the details, a Han Clan elder rushed over to Aunt Han Jiu and cried, "Aunt Jiu! Back then, Emperor Qing was unfair, and we couldn't do anything about it. Now that Emperor Qing has fallen, we ask you to make the decision for the Han Clan! "
Hearing this, Aunt Han Jiu, Han Liusu, and Han Chuyue's hearts trembled.
"Emperor Qing … is he dead?" Aunt Han Jiu asked.
"Yes! Emperor Qing's connection to the universe collapsed, "the elder cried.
Luo Zheng said, "I was the one who shattered Emperor Hanqing's universe."
Before killing Emperor Qing, Luo Zheng had some misgivings.
But such a person wasn't fit to be a father. Even in death, he had used his own daughter as a hostage. Luo Zheng couldn't find a single reason to forgive him.
Han Liusu pursed her lips and shook her head. "Father only has himself to blame …"
"Then what about brother?" Han Chuyue knew that Han Cangyan was still under Emperor Qing's control. She was naturally worried about his safety.
"Emperor Qing sent Han Cangyan to Saint Mountain to kill me. I erased his soul imprint. He's here."
As Luo Zheng spoke, he waved his hand and Han Cangyan's body appeared in front of everyone. However, Han Cangyan's soul had no memories. He could only be considered a walking corpse.

Chapter 2591
Translator: Transn Editor: Transn
Revised Rules
Li Luoshui nodded. With a flip of her finger, the reincarnation soul tool appeared in her hand.
As she closed her eyes and her eyelashes trembled, her consciousness began to wander in the sea of memories.
Using the reincarnation soul tool, she quickly found Han Cangyan's memories.
"Feng!"
Everyone's memories were different in color. A faint orange flame floated above the reincarnation soul tool.
Han Liusu and Han Chuyue stared at the memories, their eyes filled with nervousness.
An elder brother was like a father. Emperor Han Qing's death was his own fault, but they still needed a pillar in their hearts. Aunt Han Jiu might be able to become their pillar, but she couldn't compare to Han Cangyan.
"Hu …"
The faint orange flame floated towards Han Cangyan's mind.
After a long time, Han Cangyan slowly opened his eyes. Looking at the people around him, his face was filled with confusion.
"Liusu, what happened to me …"
As he spoke, he suddenly felt a strange feeling coming from his fingertips. The ring in his hand was actually emitting a surge of power. This power was so strong that it immediately made his eyes widen. This ring allowed him to feel the existence of the entire Abyss Devil Domain!
"What's with this ring? What happened?" Han Cangyan almost couldn't help but want to throw the ring out.
What happened after Han Cangyan's memories were removed was a long story. Han Liusu sighed and said, "Big brother, come to my side. I'll tell you …"
After Han Cangyan was called away, Dongfang Yingqing endured for a long time before walking out of the crowd and asking, "Luo Zheng, how is Tianming now?"
Dongfang Yingqing knew that Saint Mountain hadn't closed, and the competition for the position of Saint was still ongoing.
In Huan Yu, Yu Taibai had told her that Hua Tianming had a high chance of obtaining the position of Saint, so she didn't have to worry.
But Dongfang Yingqing could imagine how fierce the competition for the position of Saint was, so she couldn't help but ask.
Hearing Dongfang Yingqing's words, Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of shame.
Before Luo Zheng could speak, Dongfang Yingqing's heart tightened. She asked, "Did something happen to him?"
"Brother Tianming has fallen in the Saint Mountain," Luo Zheng replied.
At this moment, Luo Xiao's expression was very serious. Hua Tianming and Chen Huangyi Sword both came from the Great Yan Universe. Luo Xiao could instantly know whether they were dead or alive. Therefore, when Luo Xiao was surrounded by the Moonlight Slaughterer in the outer realm, he knew that Hua Tianming had fallen.
But Luo Xiao didn't know the details. He planned to ask Luo Zheng in detail later, but he didn't expect Dongfang Yingqing to ask first in a hurry.
Dongfang Yingqing's ruddy complexion instantly turned deathly pale, and tears welled up in her eyes. Her lips trembled, and she was unable to utter a single word …
For so many years, every time Hua Tianming left, she would fall into a state of worry, but he never disappointed her. He was blessed with great fortune, and when he returned, he was always safe and sound, even when he went to the Abyss Devil Domain.
But this time, the thing she was most worried about finally happened.
Looking at Dongfang Yingqing's expression, Luo Zheng hurriedly said, "Although he's dead, we have the fire of his memories! We can let him reincarnate in the Divine Realm. With Tianming's talent, he can cultivate to become an upper-level True God in at most 50 years! "
Dongfang Yingqing was slightly taken aback. She, who was originally in grief, suddenly had a glimmer of hope. "You … can let Tianming reincarnate?"
Luo Zheng nodded firmly. "My mother controls the land of reincarnation in the Divine Realm. She can know about Hua Tianming's reincarnated physical body, and she can also return Brother Tianming's memories to him!"
A mere few decades was extremely short for a True God. Dongfang Yingqing naturally could afford to wait.
"But the fire of memories should only contain Hua Tianming's memories in the Divine Realm. The memories in the Great Yan Universe …" Luo Zheng said as he looked at Luo Xiao. "I wonder if Father can retrieve them from the universe."
Everyone's memories in the Divine Realm were stored in the land of reincarnation. However, the universe was ultimately incomparable to the Divine Realm. Luo Zheng couldn't confirm this matter, so he could only ask Luo Xiao now.
Luo Xiao pondered for a moment before saying, "I can't retrieve the memories of every living being in the Great Yan Universe. However, it shouldn't be a problem if it's just Hua Tianming."
Hearing his father's answer, Luo Zheng heaved a sigh of relief.
Although Brother Tianming needed to cultivate from the beginning, he was a God's citizen the moment he was born in the Divine Realm. Moreover, with his memories preserved, his cultivation would be twice as fast with half the effort. Moreover, there were so many people backing Hua Tianming. The situation was better than he had imagined.
…
…
Three days later, the Saint Mountain on the northern shore of the Sea of Time suddenly trembled.
Dozens of quasi-sages and Perfect True Gods came out of the gate in the Saint Mountain.
These quasi-sages and Perfect True Gods' auras were declining, and their faces were filled with regret and disappointment. The remaining three holy springs were occupied by the Leng Family, the Xie Family, and the Bai Family respectively.
Although they were unwilling, they also rejoiced at the same time.
As long as they were alive, there was still hope to be canonized as a Saint. Those quasi-sages and Perfect True Gods who had died had no hope.
"Rumble …"
As the Saint Mountain continued to tremble, the mountain began to slowly descend and sink into the ground.
The huge mountain sank down and rolled out a huge pit in the ground. The seawater of the Sea of Time also poured in from the other side of the pit.
For countless years in the Divine Realm, the Saint Mountain had been enveloped by the Sea of Time's seawater, suppressing the nascent will of the Divine Realm. Now that the nascent will had been taken away, the Saint Mountain still had a mission to be canonized as a Saint.
After the Saint Mountain returned to its original position, a few rays of light suddenly erupted in the sky above the Divine Realm.
Those rays of light shot straight toward the Saint Hall. Not long after, there were five more figures in the Saint Hall. The two people in the lead were Patriarch Qian Long and Chen Huang Yijian.
When their inner worlds became spirit lodging grounds, they could obtain an additional portion of power from the Divine Realm.
In the eyes of the Nirvana Realm powerhouses, they were still Perfect True Gods, but their true strength was much more powerful than Perfect True Gods.
These five people's faces were filled with joy. From now on, they could be considered to be standing at the peak of the Divine Realm.
Especially the new Saint of the Leng family, Leng Wuyu, who was full of joy. After being canonized as a Saint, he could be on equal footing with the Saint Emperor of the Leng family, Leng Yao!
At this moment, a figure quietly appeared in the Saint Hall. It was Luo Zheng.
Following Luo Zheng were Luo Xiao, Saint Jiu, Li Luoshui, and the others.
"Master!"
Chen Huang Yijian, who had been canonized as a Saint, still bowed respectfully to Luo Xiao.
Leng Wuyu and the other Saints coldly glanced at Luo Zheng.
This Luo Zheng had suppressed everyone in the Saint Mountain by himself, but this guy was still only a True God.
Although Leng Wuyu and the others were afraid of Luo Zheng's strength, they still thought that they were a level higher than Luo Zheng.
